# Oanarina UI Tool — feature register

2022 individually named requirements. 1992 required baseline items; 30 later Oanarina improvements.

This is a working parity specification, not a claim of completeness or of implemented Figma compatibility. Reference links identify category-level starting points; each requirement marked reference-verification-pending still needs an in-app or documentation check. Several OS integration, reliability, and cross-product requirements are Oanarina requirements rather than verified Figma functions. Figma Design is the first implementation target; Draw, Dev Mode, FigJam, Slides, Sites, Buzz and Make remain in scope in later phases. Features introduced by a future Figma update require another audit.

Status: {"implemented":1591,"partial":232,"planned":13,"retired":186}. **planned** = not implemented; **retired** = deliberately out of scope for an offline Mac app; **partial** = material limits documented; **implemented** = working path with validation evidence. Production parity requires additional accessibility, performance, platform and user acceptance testing even for implemented items. Binary .fig Design import supports a documented subset; binary export remains planned.

Machine-readable source: public/features.json. Regenerate this document with npm run inventory.

## Mac system integration

| ID | Required behavior | Status | Verification / limits |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| UI-02-001 | Menu accelerator labels | implemented | All registry shortcuts appear: 49 as real key equivalents, 22 single-key ones as display-only labels. Shortcuts a person adds in keyboard preferences do not appear in native menus. |
| UI-02-002 | System font access | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-02-003 | Font installation detection | partial | Core Text and NSFont change notifications refresh the font list and Missing Fonts without reopening. Tested with process-scope register and unregister only, not Font Book. |
| UI-02-004 | Trackpad two-finger panning | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-02-005 | Trackpad pinch zoom | partial | Safari gesture events zoom the canvas around the pointer with the wheel path's limits. Tested with synthetic DOM events on real WKWebView; no physical trackpad. |
| UI-02-006 | Mouse wheel zoom | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-02-007 | Middle-button panning | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-02-008 | Spacebar hand navigation | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-02-009 | Context menus | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-02-010 | System clipboard text | implemented | External plain text, including multi-line Unicode, pastes as an editable layer up to 100,000 characters; rich-text-only pasteboards paste their words. Styling from other apps is not kept. Focused-field system paste was not retested because smokes avoid the real clipboard. docs/NATIVE-CLIPBOARD.md |
| UI-02-011 | System clipboard images | implemented | Mac: PNG, TIFF, JPEG, HEIC, WebP and GIF normalize to PNG, and Finder-copied image files paste their real pixels, not the icon. Only the first item or file is read; browser and Windows clipboards are out of scope. |
| UI-02-012 | Clipboard SVG markup | implemented | Outgoing public.svg-image; incoming SVG representations, whole-document SVG plain text and Finder-copied SVG all reach the validated import review. Slide containers still refuse SVG paste. |
| UI-02-013 | Drag files from Finder | implemented | Finder drops tested through WKWebView's real drag destination with a test NSDraggingInfo: images land under the pointer with one Undo, batches work, TIFF and HEIC convert to PNG. SVG, PDF and Sketch reach their reviews, and .oui opens in its own window through the shell (.fig shares that shell path but was not dropped in a smoke). Mixed drops are refused. Slides and the Files screen take no images. A person's physical drag was not exercised. |
| UI-02-014 | Drag assets to Finder | partial | The Export dialog preview drags as WebKit's own NSFilePromiseProvider, named by the shell, with no new message; the promise's real write produced the exported image. Canvas layers and Assets cannot be dragged. Finder's side of the drop was not exercised. |
| UI-02-015 | Quick Look previews | planned | reference-verification-pending |
| UI-02-016 | Spotlight document metadata | planned | reference-verification-pending |
| UI-02-017 | macOS document versions | partial | Save stores the replaced bytes via NSFileVersion; File → Revert To restores one as an undoable, unsaved edit. Not NSDocument, no Browse All Versions UI, and versions are recorded only on Save. |
| UI-02-018 | Reopen closed documents | implemented | v0.2.0-dev.6: reachable workflow with appropriate core/browser/native checks and persistence/undo where applicable. See docs/VALIDATION-v0.2-grid-files-windows.md. Exact Figma reference acceptance remains separate. Native Reopen Closed Window restores the last closed window from its own local recovery identity. Availability depends on browser database retention. See docs/MAC-WINDOWS.md. |
| UI-02-019 | macOS print dialog | implemented | Print presents the real macOS print sheet (PMPrintPanelView) and a real job produced PDF pages. Output is flattened 2× raster, so text is not vector. |
| UI-02-020 | Export through system share sheet | partial | Share opens the system picker with a private temporary 2× PNG or PDF, and macOS proposes services. No service carried through to delivery; no format choice. |
| UI-02-021 | Accent color preferences | partial | Eight local accent presets, stored outside .oui, pass contrast checks in both themes. The macOS system accent is not read, and some older chrome keeps fixed violet values. |
| UI-02-022 | System appearance following | implemented | Actual WKWebView and editor theme follow an appearance change while running, in both directions. Driven by NSApp.appearance, not by changing System Settings. |
| UI-02-023 | Reduced motion preference | partial | prefers-reduced-motion in WKWebView equals the macOS setting as it stood (off); the setting was not toggled. Earlier limits stand. |
| UI-02-024 | Increased contrast preference | partial | prefers-contrast in WKWebView equals the macOS setting as it stood (off); the setting was not toggled. Canvas selection keeps its own option. |
| UI-02-025 | VoiceOver integration | partial | Working basic path with limits; see docs/IMPLEMENTATION.md. Full reference behavior remains incomplete. |
| UI-02-026 | Keyboard focus ring | implemented | Web focus ring as in UI-44-012, rendered by web content in the shell. Native Tab reachability under macOS keyboard settings is not verified. |
| UI-02-027 | App sandbox file access | planned | reference-verification-pending |
| UI-02-028 | Offline startup | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-02-029 | App update checking | retired | Retired October 31, 2026: Forma is an offline Mac app, so update checks over the network are out of scope. |
| UI-02-030 | Update release notes | retired | Retired October 31, 2026: Forma is an offline Mac app, so update checks over the network are out of scope. |
| UI-02-031 | Editable design clipboard between Mac windows | implemented | v0.2.0-dev.8: explicit native Copy/Cut/Paste transfers validated editable Forma layers between document windows with remapped definitions, used embeddable fonts, source context, one-step Undo and portable saving. Native two-WKWebView/private-pasteboard acceptance passed. 20 MB/20k-layer limits, referenced-main Cut refusal and supported closure limits: docs/NATIVE-CLIPBOARD.md. No private Figma clipboard claim. |

No hosted sharing or cross-page prototype links. docs/PAGES.md. |
| UI-05-022 | Page-level developer status | implemented | Inspect → Development handoff marks or resets the current page as ready/completed; independent page metadata persists with Undo and legacy absence is preserved. Cross-page handoff search and real navigation are available. File-local status only; reference/native verification pending. See docs/LOCAL-HANDOFF-STATUS.md. |
| UI-05-023 | Page identifier persistence | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-05-024 | Document color profile | implemented | File information assigns or converts. Convert rewrites paints, styles, palette, variables, keyframes and shader colors so appearance holds (browser pixels within 3 of 255), after a review that counts clipping, in one Undo. Values stay 8-bit; a round trip can move up to 2 steps; images stay; files with linked libraries refuse. |
| UI-05-025 | sRGB document profile | implemented | sRGB is the default. It is stored as absence of the key, so legacy files and output are unchanged. |
| UI-05-026 | Display P3 document profile | partial | Canvas uses CSS color(display-p3); PNG and JPG are rendered on a P3 canvas and tagged. SVG uses CSS P3 colors. PDF is converted to sRGB (pixel-tested), and GIF, video, clipboard and slides use the same default without a color test. In WebKit, pattern and feImage paints clip to sRGB. Panel swatches stay sRGB previews. |
| UI-05-027 | File description | implemented | v0.2.0-dev.9: File information edits an optional 10,000-unit plain-text .oui description through explicit Save/Cancel/Clear, one Undo and stale guards. Legacy/CRLF/no-op, actual saved open/recovery/snapshot restore and MCP metadata/redaction have coverage; global editor drafts validate before commit. Description is not mapped through Figma Bridge. docs/FILE-INFORMATION.md. |
| UI-05-028 | File cover selection | partial | Unreleased source: Reachable any-page frame cover picker/search/reset, isolated cached grid/list images with authored viewport/full-page clipping/masks/background/visible overlap, render-only IDs, Undo/save/reopen and deletion/move lifecycle. Optional oui1/2 metadata validated before mutation.500-layer/4MB UTF-8 preview budgets, static posters and conservative cached-font conflict diagnostics; native/Figma thumbnail parity remains unverified. docs/DOCUMENT-COVER-SELECTION.md. |
| UI-05-029 | File-level library metadata | implemented | File information shows publication state, version count and libraries used, and edits owner, license, contact and tags as validated plain text, with Undo, .oui persistence and copying into snapshots shown in consumer review. Implementation validated; reference verification pending. |
| UI-05-030 | File naming from title | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |

| UI-10-024 | Slice export naming | implemented | Unreleased source: Manager renames slices with an explicit guarded Save/Enter action and edits per-profile filename suffixes. Manager name editing is bounded to 512 characters while longer legacy names remain intact. docs/SLICE-EXPORT-WORKFLOWS.md. Native/exact-reference acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-10-025 | Toggle slice visibility | implemented | Unreleased source: View, Preferences and slice manager toggle local canvas-only outlines/labels; border/label targets leave interior artwork clickable. Separate saved slice layer visibility updates outlines/visible batch eligibility with Undo. Hidden/inherited visibility and affine/Motion geometry apply; global toggle leaves artwork/history/exports unchanged and migrates older preferences. docs/SLICE-EXPORT-WORKFLOWS.md. Native/exact-reference acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-10-026 | Export overlapping slices | implemented | Unreleased source: Visible current-page slices export independently in a ZIP through each crop inverse affine transform. Tests decode actual overlapping, reflected/nonuniform transformed PNG crops at different scales. All overlapping artwork keeps mask/clip context; slices and their imported subtrees never paint or orphan export nodes. Snapshot exports preserve saved artwork. docs/SLICE-EXPORT-WORKFLOWS.md. Native/exact-reference acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-10-027 | Select slice through panel | implemented | Unreleased source: Dedicated bounded/searchable slice list supports arrow/Home/End navigation across 50-entry pages, explicit inspection of hidden/locked regions, and Select slice on canvas with returned focus. Existing Layers selection remains. Manager is page-local, with 4096-character name-prefix search; full names remain saved. docs/SLICE-EXPORT-WORKFLOWS.md. Native/exact-reference acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-10-028 | Remove slice | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-10-029 | Slice export scale | implemented | Unreleased source: Per-slice saved export scale 0.01×–64× and aspect-preserving fixed pixel width/height use the shared profile pipeline. Browser tests verify actual PNG dimensions/pixels and scaled SVG dimensions, Undo/save and stale-input refusal. Raster edges 16384px/80M pixels; bounds remain the slice rectangle. docs/SLICE-EXPORT-WORKFLOWS.md. Native/exact-reference acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-10-030 | Slice SVG export | implemented | Unreleased source: Slice SVG profiles download actual fixed-region cropped artwork in slice-local axes, with supported text/appearance, layer-ID and presentation options. Real scaled SVG filenames/dimensions, no slice-overlay paint and immutable source are tested. Uses existing renderer/fonts/media limitations; no binary .fig export. docs/SLICE-EXPORT-WORKFLOWS.md. Native/exact-reference acceptance remains pending. |

Optional normalized metadata preserves oui1/2 and legacy center rendering. In-flow auto-layout compensation refuses; multi-selection still uses selection center. Bridge explicitly omits editing metadata with existing pose limits. docs/CUSTOM-ROTATION-ORIGIN.md. Exact Figma reference/native acceptance remains pending. |

Exact Figma reference verification remains pending. |

| UI-14-025 | Bend tool | implemented | Unreleased source: reachable Bend segments mode in point editing drags straight/cubic segments or midpoint handles, including closing segments, through parent/path transforms. Fixed anchors and existing node bounds remain; two independent tangent handles use bounded least-squares displacement with grabbed t clamped to .08–.92. Control provides finer movement; guarded preview/commit/cancel, one Undo, save/reopen and exported outward-curve pixels are tested. ±1,000,000 path/grab limits; Motion/managed/locked/collapsed paths refused. Artwork exports conservatively include control hulls. See docs/VECTOR-BEND.md. Native/reference acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-14-026 | Vector network branches | implemented | Graph version 3 holds any planar network: multiple cycles, faces and holes. Crossings become explicit junctions when edges are created or contours converted; moving a vertex or handle across an edge still refuses. Works under effects, masks, auto layout, instance copies and layer Motion, with any centered stroke stack; Smart Animate and Bridge refuse. docs/VECTOR-NETWORK-FACES.md. Older builds refuse version 3 files. |
| UI-14-027 | Fill bounded vector region | implemented | Every bounded face can be picked on the canvas and given its own stack of 1–16 solid or gradient paints, with holes, Undo, save and SVG pixels tested. Image and shader paints on a region refuse. Region paints do not appear in selection colors or asset usage. |
| UI-14-028 | Remove vector region fill | implemented | Remove region fill works per face and keeps the paint for refill. Deleting a boundary merges faces or removes the region in one Undo instead of refusing; a split gives both parts the paint. |

Undo/save and interrupted-gesture validation Evidence and limits: docs/VECTOR-WORKFLOWS.md; tests/vector-workflows.test.ts; tests/e2e/vector-workflows.spec.ts. Exact Figma reference verification remains pending. |

| UI-14-031 | Exit vector edit mode | implemented | v0.2 development: inspector/command, .oui persistence and undo; core tests and browser workflows documented in docs/VALIDATION-v0.2-development.md. Cubic pen, anchor/handle editing and persisted curves have browser coverage. |
| UI-14-032 | Round vector corners | implemented | Round selected corners fits a circular fillet tangent to straight or cubic neighbors and keeps exact de Casteljau curve pieces, with one Undo, save/reopen and SVG export. It is baked geometry rather than a live per-point radius, is capped at half each adjoining segment and 4,096 corners per request, and refuses smooth anchors, cusps, duplicates, open endpoints and over-tight curves atomically. Exact Figma reference and native acceptance remain pending. docs/VECTOR-WORKFLOWS.md; tests/vector-corner-rounding.test.ts; tests/e2e/vector-corner-rounding.spec.ts. |
| UI-14-033 | Flatten vector network | implemented | Flatten keeps the picture: unfilled graphs give one open contour per edge, one paint stack stays on the layer with an even-odd fill, and each further stack becomes a fill layer below the stroke, in one Undo. |
| UI-14-034 | Outline vector stroke | partial | Gradient and image strokes now outline with their paint moved to the fill, though the paint maps to a slightly larger box and can shift a little. They need no visible fill alongside, and SVG export still refuses them. Multiple strokes and arrowheads stay unsupported. |
| UI-14-035 | Simplify vector path | implemented | v0.2 development: inspector/command, .oui persistence and undo; core tests and browser workflows documented in docs/VALIDATION-v0.2-development.md. Cubic pen, anchor/handle editing and persisted curves have browser coverage. |
| UI-14-036 | Paste SVG path data | implemented | Unreleased source: Reachable path endpoint continuation/closing, corner/smooth and segment controls, transformed point marquee/coordinates, winding, validated SVG path replacement and layer switching. Undo/save and interrupted-gesture validation Evidence and limits: docs/VECTOR-WORKFLOWS.md; tests/vector-workflows.test.ts; tests/e2e/vector-workflows.spec.ts. Exact Figma reference verification remains pending. |
| UI-14-037 | Smooth join vector endpoints | implemented | v0.2.0-dev.3: reachable authoring/playback path, undo/persistence and validation where applicable. Core/browser/native evidence and limits: docs/VALIDATION-v0.2-prototypes-workspaces.md. Reference acceptance remains separate. Endpoint joins accept two open sibling layers or two selected endpoints. Splitting keeps separate contours in one layer; point transforms normalize bounds. |
| UI-14-038 | Delete vector points and heal path | implemented | Delete and heal uses a tangent-preserving cubic through the real point tools, with degenerate remnants refused. Existing tests cover it; exact Figma curve fitting stays a reference matter. |
| UI-14-039 | Offset vector path | implemented | Unreleased source: Reachable Vector menu/point-tool offset dialog previews original/proposed real SVG snapshots with isolated definitions and ancestor masks/clips. Closed contours expand/contract including holes/concavity; open paths shift to a signed parallel path. One guarded Undo/save/reopen transaction. Approximate cubic flattening tolerance 0.25 parent units, 32 contours/512 input or flattened points/4096 output points; mixed open/closed, crossing/touching/reversed/collapsed geometry refuses atomically. docs/VECTOR-OFFSET.md. Native/exact-reference acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-14-040 | Offset distance control | implemented | Unreleased source: Signed numerical offset distance controls real geometry and previews; positive/negative semantics and parent units are stated. Finite nonzero magnitude 0.001–10000 is validated; stale document/page/selection/mode/Motion/camera/blur contexts refuse Apply. Actual exported pixels and one-history persistence are tested. This offsets paths rather than stroke width. docs/VECTOR-OFFSET.md. Native/exact-reference acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-14-041 | Offset square or round joins | implemented | Offset now offers Miter (limit 4, bevel fallback), Round and a true clipped Square join, which cuts straight across at exactly the offset distance from the anchor on outer corners, in closed and open paths, with real preview, Apply and saved geometry. Curves are still flattened to 0.25 units and the existing geometry, topology and size limits apply. Exact Figma join equivalence remains unverified. docs/VECTOR-OFFSET.md. Square joins are also tested on curved closed and open paths; on flattened curves square and round differ by under 1% of area, and only real corners differ visibly. |
| UI-14-042 | Vector cut tool | partial | Segment scissors are unchanged, and a separate straight Knife slices filled shapes. No freehand or partial knife, no general networks, no anchor cuts. |
| UI-14-043 | Slice vectors into separate layers | partial | Knife slices rectangles, ellipses, polygons, stars and closed paths into editable pieces in an identity-preserving group, with one Undo. The line extends across the whole layer; solid fills only, no visible strokes, 512 anchors and 32 pieces. docs/VECTOR-SEPARATION.md. |
| UI-14-044 | Vector eraser tool | partial | The eraser now removes brushed area from closed filled shapes, which can bite, open holes or split contours. Round brushes make short straight edges, the layer box is not tightened, and erasing everything is refused. Networks and open filled paths stay unsupported. |
| UI-14-045 | Vector eraser size and shape | implemented | Unreleased source: Reachable eraser controls select round or square brush and diameter1–500 canvas units. Continuous sweeps, cursor/preview agreement, bounded 64-turn prefix and explicit freeze feedback, interruption/pointer/context guards and actual browser checks. Stroke-centerline erasure scope and whole-path refusal documented. docs/VECTOR-ERASER-LASSO.md. Exact Figma reference/native acceptance remains pending. |

## Freehand and Figma Draw

| ID | Required behavior | Status | Verification / limits |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| UI-16-001 | Pencil freehand drawing | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |

| UI-16-003 | Pencil path editing | implemented | v0.2 development: inspector/command, .oui persistence and undo; core tests and browser workflows documented in docs/VALIDATION-v0.2-development.md. |
| UI-16-004 | Brush drawing | partial | Primitive, sampled, scatter and stretch brushes draw editable output with draft cancellation and one Undo. Native tablet acceptance and uncertain clipped or masked container ownership remain open. |
| UI-16-005 | Brush preset picker | implemented | Unreleased source: Reachable five stock presets and persistent local custom picker with actual rendered settings; built-ins are read-only and customization is independent. No Figma preset/library parity claim. docs/VECTOR-BRUSH-DRAWING.md. |
| UI-16-006 | Vector brush library | partial | Local library holds primitive, sampled, scatter and stretch presets with bounded JSON import and export. Hosted or shared libraries are absent. |
| UI-16-007 | Custom brush creation | partial | Named brushes can be primitive, sampled or stretch and persist locally. Multi-color art is absent. |
| UI-16-008 | Brush spacing | implemented | Unreleased source: Spacing percent controls actual nominal-size arc-length intervals, including continuous remainder across input/coalesced segments and interpolated pressure at each stamp. Bounded strokes refuse atomically without a hidden prefix. docs/VECTOR-BRUSH-DRAWING.md. |
| UI-16-009 | Brush direction | implemented | Unreleased source: Fixed or follow-stroke tangent direction plus angle control changes actual stamp transforms, including captured signed/nonuniform parent mapping. Native/reference acceptance pending. docs/VECTOR-BRUSH-DRAWING.md. |
| UI-16-010 | Brush size | implemented | Unreleased source: Reachable size/aspect controls change editable stamp geometry and shared preview/export; captured settings, bounds and cancellation prevent stale gesture commits. docs/VECTOR-BRUSH-DRAWING.md. |
| UI-16-011 | Brush pressure response | partial | Unreleased source: Actual supplied pen/touch pressure interpolates size/opacity response and configurable minimum into saved stamps; mouse uses full pressure and release zero does not collapse the end interval. Real Chromium pen input tested; physical tablet and Mac WKWebView pressure acceptance remain unverified. docs/VECTOR-BRUSH-DRAWING.md. |
| UI-16-012 | Brush opacity | implemented | Unreleased source: Opacity control changes each materialized stamp, preserving overlapping alpha compositing in shared Canvas/SVG/masks; pressure opacity can additionally vary stamps. Presets and completed artwork persist independently. docs/VECTOR-BRUSH-DRAWING.md. |
| UI-16-013 | Variable width strokes | implemented | Curves and closed contours are native: handles stay on the document, and the band follows the cubic envelope with widths along real length. Miter joins, gradients and asymmetric width bias work; dashes do not. Older builds refuse curved or closed profiles and miter or gradient profiles, and ignore widthBias. docs/VARIABLE-WIDTH-STROKES.md. |
| UI-16-014 | Width control points | implemented | Width points are added numerically or by clicking the stroke, by exact split, so the line does not move. Shift-drag or Anchor width bias % gives asymmetric widths. Width points are anchors, not a separate track. |
| UI-16-015 | Dynamic stroke wiggle | partial | Wave runs on open and closed curves, with exact splits and a periodic seam. Effects, masks, instances and Motion are valid; live Booleans refuse. No Smoothen control, no network support, and it is a sine, not Figma's noise. |
| UI-16-016 | Dynamic stroke frequency | implemented | Integer frequency 1–16 works on the same curved and closed geometry, with the same exact sampling. Undersized spacing and the 512-anchor budget refuse. |
| UI-16-017 | Stroke taper | implemented | Taper presets gain amount, smooth and keep-existing options. Stamped brushes gain start and end taper by real stroke length, floored at 5% of size. Stretch brushes have no taper (refused). |
| UI-16-018 | Calligraphic stroke | partial | Nib sweeps curved and closed contours, and effects, masks, instances and Motion are valid. Pressure and tilt remain absent; auto layout and live Booleans refuse. |
| UI-16-019 | Text on path | implemented | Closed baselines are native, and text passes the seam at any offset. 1–16 contours act as continuing baselines. Rich ranges and RTL work; effects, masks, instances and layer Motion are valid. Auto layout, live Booleans, Smart Animate, Bridge and SVG <textPath> import still refuse. Older builds refuse the new forms; earlier saved loops still open. |
| UI-16-020 | Text path offset | implemented | Start offset has a numeric field and a canvas handle that drags along the real baseline with live glyph preview, Escape cancel and one Undo on release, and Flip text direction reverses the baseline. Exact Figma control equivalence is unverified. docs/TEXT-ON-PATH.md. |
| UI-16-021 | Shape builder tool | partial | Fill-only networks are now sources, and network regions merge with neighbours in the network inspector. Same-color opaque unstroked sources are still required, and the very-fast-drag note stands. |
| UI-16-022 | Lasso vector selection | implemented | Unreleased source: Actual freehand anchor lasso within one contour path; concave/self-crossing even-odd selection, inclusive boundaries, Shift add/Option subtract, signed-affine cached anchor projection, bounded outline and invalidatable pointer/context guards. Selection-only operation leaves document/history untouched; up to256 vertices/10000 anchors. docs/VECTOR-ERASER-LASSO.md. Exact Figma reference/native acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-16-023 | Multi-vector editing | partial | Multi-vector anchor drags now snap to geometry outside the moving contours (handles do not). Shared networks, cross-layer topology and anchor-fragment Cut remain absent. |
| UI-16-024 | Pattern fills | partial | Unreleased source: Editable local pattern tile source with up to64 bounded rect/ellipse/line primitives, add/delete/order/duplicate/size/position/rotation/colors/opacity, staged source preview/Apply one Undo, full-scene shared SVG rendering, validated canonical snapshots and local persistence. Arbitrary live document-layer pattern sources remain absent. docs/PATTERN-TEXTURE-NOISE-FILLS.md. Exact Figma reference/native acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-16-025 | Pattern scale | implemented | Unreleased source: Reachable surface scale percentage controls repeat editable patterns/noise/local textures at1–6400%, bounded by0.25–65536px tile periods. Shared canvas/SVG/export/masks/styles/component/clipboard validation, no-op preservation, Undo/reopen. docs/PATTERN-TEXTURE-NOISE-FILLS.md. Exact Figma reference/native acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-16-026 | Pattern rotation | implemented | Unreleased source: Surface rotation changes the complete gap-free repeating lattice around its source origin with bounded angle, shared SVG/canvas/export coordinates, opacity/mask/affine tests, Undo and saved reopening. docs/PATTERN-TEXTURE-NOISE-FILLS.md. Exact Figma reference/native acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-16-027 | Repeat pattern offsets | implemented | Unreleased source: Repeat X/Y offsets translate the complete surface lattice without tile gaps, retaining rotation/scale and shared canvas/export/mask coordinates; explicit reset, Undo and persistence. docs/PATTERN-TEXTURE-NOISE-FILLS.md. Exact Figma reference/native acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-16-028 | Noise fill | implemented | Unreleased source: Seeded deterministic raster noise fill with monochrome/two-color or independent RGB channels, coverage/transparent pixels, tile size/grain scale, colors and seed controls; pure lossless PNG snapshot generation, validated canonical metadata and Undo/save. This is bounded repeating raster grain, not a shader. docs/PATTERN-TEXTURE-NOISE-FILLS.md. Exact Figma reference/native acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-16-029 | Texture fill | implemented | Unreleased source: Embedded still PNG/JPEG/WebP texture fill chooser/replacement, intrinsic pixel tiling, scale/rotation/phase/opacity and image adjustments. Existing decoder/signature/dimension/byte limits, delayed-file context guards, no external URLs, Undo/save/PNG/SVG preservation. Source-MCP image-asset upload remains separate. docs/PATTERN-TEXTURE-NOISE-FILLS.md. Exact Figma reference/native acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-16-030 | Progressive blur | partial | Unreleased source: Real spatially varying nine-level Gaussian blend, start/end radii, projected direction/extents and actual shared Artwork/SVG/raster/masks, bounded subtree overflow, styles/components/clipboard/Undo/save/source MCP. Strict metadata/count/region budgets; Canvas drafts, geometry/text Motion, prototype state/Smart Animate and font-time bounds have explicit safe refusal/recovery; source/history remain unchanged. Exact continuous/native effect and Figma round-trip parity absent; Bridge refuses new effect. docs/PROGRESSIVE-BLUR.md; docs/PROGRESSIVE-FONT-SAFETY.md. |
| UI-16-031 | Brush import | partial | Forma brush JSON imports validate before use, including the new stretch field. Third-party and Figma brush formats are unsupported. |
| UI-16-032 | Brush export | partial | Selected-brush and whole-library JSON export includes stretch settings. No third-party format. |
| UI-16-033 | Brush reset | implemented | Unreleased source: Reset restores the selected saved stock/custom preset without changing completed document artwork; source settings and custom presets persist locally. Document Undo is reserved for materialized strokes. docs/VECTOR-BRUSH-DRAWING.md. |
| UI-16-034 | Illustration mode toolbar | implemented | Draw mode adds a Brush presets menu and an Illustration tools menu reaching Edit points, Bend, Cut, Erase, Lasso, Knife, Shape builder, Offset, Outline stroke, Separate regions and Linear/Radial repeat, with toolbar keyboard support and on-screen checks at 1100, 900 and 760 px. View state only, no separate Draw inspector, and the entries are not registered commands, so they are absent from Actions search. docs/STRETCH-BRUSHES.md. |
| UI-16-035 | Radial repeat | implemented | Keep live stores one layer whose copies follow its edits and Motion, with export bounds, editable count, pivot, angle and rotation, Expand to layers and Remove. Copies are not individually editable until expanded. Not allowed as a mask, Boolean or instance child, directly in auto layout, or nested; limit 4,096 copies per page. Bridge refuses; an older build shows only the source. |
| UI-16-036 | Linear repeat | implemented | Same live repeat with count and X/Y steps, under the same limits. |
| UI-16-037 | Stretch brush creation | implemented | Sampled vector brushes can stretch one silhouette along the whole stroke as one ordinary editable path, with holes kept, pressure on thickness only, one Undo, save and export. Art is warped by bounded cubic approximation, tight turns can fold a thick band, and there is no tiling or fixed end caps. Preset JSON gains an optional stretch field; .oui is unchanged. docs/STRETCH-BRUSHES.md. |
| UI-16-038 | Scatter brush creation | partial | Deterministic scatter is unchanged. Continuous warp now exists only as the separate stretch brush, and a preset cannot be both. |
| UI-16-039 | Sample a brush from canvas | partial | A sampled silhouette can now become a stamp or a stretch brush. Source limits are unchanged. |

| UI-18-030 | Stroke variable binding | implemented | Each stack entry beyond the first binds its own color and width variables through its stable id (stroke@id, strokeWidth@id), so tokens follow reorder, with detach on literal edit and on removal. Older builds refuse these documents. |
| UI-18-031 | Shader stroke assignment | implemented | Shared paint controls. The texture extends under the stroke so it doesn't wrap at the box edge. In a Display P3 file, WebKit clips shader strokes to sRGB. |

| UI-19-041 | Shader version update indicator | implemented | Layers stay pinned to their version until Update shader is pressed, with an indicator for bundled and file shaders. Superseded source is retained, so pinned pixels don't change. |

| UI-22-020 | Variable font axes | partial | Faces without readable tables now get axes measured by sweeping wght, wdth, opsz, slnt and GRAD. Found on system-ui and on SF loaded as a web font. | UI-22-021 | Optical sizing | partial | opsz is measured the same way on those faces, with the same limits as UI-22-020. |

| UI-22-032 | Text style detachment | implemented | Unreleased source after dev.12: Existing local text style creation/application/detachment are now exercised through real editor save/Undo/reopen. Update from selection preserves style identity and copies shared fields, rejects non-text/stale sources, and resets rejected drafts. Detach preserves effective values; remote library editing remains separate. See docs/ASSET-USAGE.md. Source implementation validation is separate from pending exact Figma reference acceptance; download archives remain unchanged. |
| UI-22-033 | Text selection find | implemented | Text menu/Actions/Command-F searches literal base text in captured page, selected layer trees or whole file, with case/Unicode word/visibility controls, previews and reveal across pages/slides. Bounded queries/results and language-specific limitations: docs/TEXT-SEARCH.md. Core and four production browser cases; reference verification pending. |
| UI-22-034 | Text replacement | implemented | Replace one/all editable literal matches atomically, preserving unaffected rich runs and first-character replacement style, inherited locks, Undo and .oui persistence. Managed overwrites refuse; bounded growth and motion/base-text limits documented in docs/TEXT-SEARCH.md. Core and production browser validation; reference verification pending. |
| UI-22-035 | Emoji input | partial | Adds the system Edit > Emoji & Symbols item. The item is verified; insertion by a person is not. Other earlier limits stand. |
| UI-22-036 | Bidirectional text | implemented | Soft wraps carry paragraph bidi context through invisible marks (open embeddings, strong neighbours, Arabic number context), with no source change. Second-line order matches the browser's whole-paragraph layout, and the test fails with the marks removed. Native textarea caret, selection and typing were tested with Arabic and Hebrew plus Latin in Chromium. Per-paragraph direction overrides are not shown in the textarea. WKWebView is unverified. |
| UI-22-037 | Right-to-left paragraphs | implemented | Unreleased source: Layer and paragraph Auto/LTR/RTL controls preserve logical UTF-16 source and rich/paragraph overrides through Undo/save, clipboard, styles/components and source MCP. Shared layout/rendering mirrors RTL paragraph/list insets, places decimal/bullet markers, honors physical alignment and trailing ellipsis; actual Hebrew/Latin/numeric/Arabic joining pixels are tested. Inline/rich text editing uses layer direction; native textareas do not style individual paragraph overrides separately. Broader cross-wrap bidi remains partial. docs/TEXT-DIRECTION-WORKFLOWS.md. Native/exact-reference acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-22-038 | Multilingual font fallback | implemented | Per-letter glyph coverage is measured for installed, WOFF2 and TTF faces, with script names and one-click Add fallback in one Undo. It does not assign fonts automatically. A family that is the system sans default can miss a gap. Bridge omission is unchanged. |
| UI-22-039 | Text on curve baseline | implemented | Browser SVG shapes rich single-line text on the actual cubic baselines: fonts, axes, features, baseline shift, decorations. Hebrew and Arabic mixed with Latin were verified in Chromium, not WKWebView. Bounds remain glyph-cell based. |
| UI-22-040 | Text accessibility description | implemented | Guarded plain text accessibility descriptions with validation, Undo/save, component/MCP paths, SVG desc and interactive canvas/prototype accessible description. Hidden mask definitions are not exposed as named graphics. Native assistive-technology acceptance pending. See docs/TEXT-INPUT-WORKFLOWS.md. |
| UI-22-041 | Smart quotation and symbol substitution | implemented | Unreleased source: opt-in device-local smart English quotes/apostrophes and finite symbol substitutions (..., --, (c), (r), (tm)) while typing one ASCII character in Rich text. Real Unicode edits retain token-first styles, later ranges and paragraphs, synchronous caret and one session Undo/save. Paste/import/bulk replacement/IME preserve literal source; stale editing contexts discard drafts. Ordinary Typography textarea/system-wide/locale-specific replacement are outside this behavior. See docs/TEXT-SETTINGS-WORKFLOWS.md. Native/reference acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-22-042 | Emoji colon-code completion | implemented | Colon completion reaches all 1,903 catalog entries, covering every recommended Unicode 17 emoji except skin-tone variants. Generated codes come from Unicode names such as woman_rocket, not GitHub or Slack names, the 81 hand-kept codes stay first, and completion has no skin-tone control. docs/TEXT-INPUT-WORKFLOWS.md. |
| UI-22-043 | Text vertical trim | partial | Trim now survives features set to 0 and liga, clig, dlig, calt, kern, tnum, pnum, lnum, onum, frac and zero. Variable axes, small capitals, raised text and other features still fall back to normal geometry. |
| UI-22-044 | Balanced and pretty text wrapping | implemented | Unreleased source: optional layer/paragraph Normal/Balance/Pretty modes use measured rich-text word partitions with unchanged greedy line count, source ranges, direction/list gutters, ellipsis, Undo/save/styles/components/clipboard and source MCP validation. Bounded optimization retains normal fallback for unsupported whitespace, emergency breaks, auto-width, legacy inline markers or exhausted work budgets. Editable Bridge reports omission; CSS is approximate. Evidence and limits: docs/TEXT-WRAPPING.md; tests/text-wrap.test.ts; tests/e2e/text-wrap.spec.ts; mcp/tests/text-wrap.test.ts. Exact Figma/CSS algorithm equivalence and native visual acceptance remain pending. |
| UI-22-045 | Hanging quotation marks | partial | Now works in RTL, mixed-script and centered paragraphs. Justified text, lists, ellipsis tails, tabs and explicit bidi controls keep normal placement. |
| UI-22-046 | Hanging list markers | implemented | Hanging marker gutters keep wrapped continuation lines aligned to list text; mixed character ranges and per-paragraph alignment share layout/rendering. See docs/PARAGRAPH-TYPOGRAPHY.md. |
| UI-22-047 | List item spacing | implemented | List-item spacing is distinct from paragraph spacing, editable for whole text or paragraph ranges, persists/undoes and participates in layout. See docs/PARAGRAPH-TYPOGRAPHY.md. |
| UI-22-048 | Underline style control | implemented | v0.2.0-dev.3: reachable authoring/playback path, undo/persistence and validation where applicable. Core/browser/native evidence and limits: docs/VALIDATION-v0.2-prototypes-workspaces.md. Reference acceptance remains separate. Reachable selected-range text/link/underline controls and styled/plain clipboard. Exported SVG retains links; native underline rendering needs further visual acceptance. |

Bounded embedded TTF/OTF GSUB/GPOS declaration inspection distinguishes unknown metadata from absent declarations; no glyph applicability guarantee. Explicit guarded range Apply preserves unrelated effective run features, with Undo/save/reopen and stale context/draft protection. Source fragment capped at 512 UTF-16 units, declared menu at 256; WOFF2/native metadata unknown; font fallback and isolated shaping context explained. docs/OPENTYPE-FEATURE-PREVIEWS.md; core and real Lato ligature pixel/browser checks. Native and reference acceptance pending. |

See docs/TEXT-SETTINGS-WORKFLOWS.md. Exact Figma settings layout/reference acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-22-055 | Line-height unit conversion | implemented | Multiplier, percentage and real fixed-pixel leading controls, shared line layout/paragraph overrides, styled clipboard, local styles/components, SVG, source MCP and Bridge PIXELS metadata. Exact Figma reference verification remains pending. |
| UI-23-023 | Resize nested constrained frame | implemented | Nested constrained resize works through the shared solver with depth-scaled convergence. Older layouts update on the next page edit. |

| UI-23-026 | Preserve constraints on reparenting | implemented | Unreleased source: shared hierarchy moves retain all five X/Y constraint modes and world pose; constraint derivation now checks previous parent ownership before applying a resize delta to newly reparented children. Later new-parent resizing uses the retained modes. Actual tree/root/context/group workflows, one Undo/save and atomic MCP reparent+destination-resize regression are tested. Unsupported newly required skew is refused; Motion/mode/stale tree drag guards preserve source ownership. See docs/FRAME-RELATIVE-POSITIONING.md. Native/reference acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-23-027 | Stretch with layout columns | implemented | Unreleased source: Durable optional x-axis binding names direct frame/component/instance owner, column guide ID and 1-based cell span. Shared derivation tracks owner size and guide/style edits, with guarded bind/detach controls, descendant constraints, Undo/save, copy/page/component/clipboard remapping and source MCP. Fixed/absolute Design children only; incompatible hug/aspect/auto-text suspends, hard min/max clamps, missing/retyped/out-of-range guide or changed owner detaches at last geometry. docs/RESPONSIVE-GUIDE-BINDINGS.md. Native/exact-reference acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-23-028 | Stretch with layout rows | implemented | Unreleased source: Durable optional y-axis row-guide owner/ID/cell span follows real owner height and guide/style edits in shared reflow. Bind/detach, Undo/save, nested constrained descendants and reference remapping are tested including actual rendered size. Same direct-parent fixed/absolute Design scope and suspension/clamp/detach rules as columns; arbitrary nested-track relations remain unfinished. docs/RESPONSIVE-GUIDE-BINDINGS.md. Native/exact-reference acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-23-029 | Relative frame positioning | implemented | Unreleased source: dedicated Frame X/Y controls express the selected layer layout origin before its own rotation in the nearest frame/component/instance axes, through transformed intervening groups. Guarded atomic descendant translation, post-derivation validation, one Undo/save/reopen and stale drafts are tested. Limits: 1024 ancestors, ±1,000,000 coordinates; singular/managed/flow/derived/protected workspace cases refuse. Responsive guide bindings remain separate unfinished requirements. See docs/FRAME-RELATIVE-POSITIONING.md. Native/reference acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-23-030 | Responsive resize preview | implemented | Unreleased source: independently resizable Design frames preview requested or preset sizes through the shared layout, constraints, guides, text and component derivation. Real isolated before/after SVG artwork, optional overflow reveal, descendant and linked box-change summaries precede explicit Apply/Undo/save/reopen. Locked frames preview read-only. Managed/controller-owned sizes and unsupported imported hierarchy refuse; stale document/selection/mode/font contexts invalidate. Complete project payload cap 16 MB; 500 scene layers/64 ancestors; solver stability and final geometry/policy signature required. docs/RESPONSIVE-RESIZE-PREVIEW.md; core/browser pixels, grid/guide/aspect/text checks. Overflow candidates remain conservative; native and reference acceptance pending. |

Variant actions work inside customized slots. |
| UI-28-030 | Variant matching on swap | implemented | A variant change picks the exact match, else the first variant with that value, and carries property values by name and type. |
| UI-28-031 | Component slot definition | partial | Definition behavior is unchanged. Slot-bearing mains stay outside other components and instances; nested template inheritance and preferred-content constraints remain absent. |
| UI-28-032 | Editable instance slot content | partial | Customized slots now accept slot-bearing instances of other mains, with their own slots, Undo, save and copy. Main defaults, managed layers and self nesting refuse. Bounded islands remain. Older builds refuse these files. |
| UI-28-033 | Reset instance slot content | partial | Reset removes nested slot content, including nested slot-bearing instances, in one Undo. No native slot round trip. |

Source implementation validation is separate from pending exact Figma reference acceptance; download archives remain unchanged. |
| UI-29-006 | Publish library | partial | Adds collection choice and a changes-since-last-cached-version list by fingerprint. No hosted or team publishing. |
| UI-29-007 | Library publish description | implemented | Unreleased source: Reachable local publication description is prepared/reviewed and saved with immutable snapshot/history; unrelated artwork/comments are excluded. docs/LOCAL-COMPONENT-LIBRARIES.md. |
| UI-29-008 | Library version details | implemented | Unreleased source: Local immutable version details expose positive version, name, description, date and source identity; fingerprints indicate content equality, not authenticated publisher identity. docs/LOCAL-COMPONENT-LIBRARIES.md. |
| UI-29-009 | Enable library in file | implemented | Local snapshot libraries enable per file with stable IDs, Undo and persistence, up to eight libraries. |
| UI-29-010 | Disable library in file | partial | Disabled definitions now leave the Assets component browser with a hidden count, and instances stay linked. Styles and variables stay in inspector pickers. |
| UI-29-011 | Browse available libraries | partial | Adds search across cached libraries. No hosted or community discovery. |
| UI-29-012 | Search library assets | implemented | Unreleased source: Reachable case-insensitive local snapshot asset search and24-row accessible pagination filter named component/set/style/guide/token groups. docs/LOCAL-COMPONENT-LIBRARIES.md. |
| UI-29-013 | Library asset preview | partial | Set previews now show any variant. The 256-layer, geometry and SVG budgets and font-metric limits remain. |
| UI-29-014 | Library update notification | implemented | A newer imported snapshot triggers an update notice, with ignore handling and per-asset accepted versions. |
| UI-29-015 | Review library updates | partial | Adds named variable and mode review with up to 200 changed values, 24 shown. No unrestricted visual diff or merge. |
| UI-29-016 | Accept all library updates | partial | Removed variables and modes no longer always refuse: those in use need an explicit mapping or confirmation, and all apply in one Undo. A variable used by a prototype action, or a type change on one in use, still refuses. |
| UI-29-017 | Accept selected library updates | partial | Same variable decisions apply to selected updates, with the dependency closure. No cloud sync. |
| UI-29-018 | Ignore library update | implemented | Unreleased source: Reachable Ignore suppresses a specific immutable local snapshot notification while preserving manual review and cached consumers; saved bounded ignore history supports Undo. docs/LOCAL-COMPONENT-LIBRARIES.md. |
| UI-29-019 | Deprecate library component | partial | Component deprecation works in local snapshots, with no per-variant status. |
| UI-29-020 | Library analytics | partial | An explicit scan counts current references in the local project library plus the open file: files, versions, instances, layers and unused assets, up to 2,000 files. It cannot see other people or devices, .oui files never opened here, history or trends, Trash or branch copies, and the panel says so. |
| UI-29-021 | Library usage counts | partial | Now also counted across device files and for styles, variables and guides, on top of current-file instance counts. No organization, cloud or longitudinal data. |
| UI-29-022 | Library permissions | retired | Retired October 31, 2026: Forma is an offline Mac app, so shared library permissions are out of scope. |
| UI-29-023 | Organization library approval | retired | Retired October 31, 2026: Forma is an offline Mac app, so organization approval is out of scope. |
| UI-29-024 | Move component between libraries | partial | Consumer-side repair: after a source removal is accepted, a group is re-homed to a cached library with the same topology. Local IDs, instances and overrides are kept in one Undo, and the next review offers the new definition. Sources are not edited. Refuses edited definitions, an already installed target, topology mismatch and changed slot identities. |
| UI-29-025 | Swap library source | implemented | Reviewed local source replacement with atomic refusals for unsafe mappings. |
| UI-29-026 | Missing library indicator | implemented | Missing snapshots and removed groups are shown while cached artwork stays usable. |
| UI-29-027 | Style naming folders | implemented | Unreleased source after dev.12: Slash-separated style names have exact-segment folder filtering and breadcrumbs, root/nested navigation and ordinary rename with Undo/persistence. Naming folders have no separate folder-object identity; library conflict resolution remains separate. See docs/ASSET-USAGE.md. Source implementation validation is separate from pending exact Figma reference acceptance; download archives remain unchanged. |

| UI-35-022 | Share prototype link | partial | File → Export prototype file… saves a ZIP with one offline HTML player and a README, validated on load and blocked from network requests by its page policy. There is no hosted URL; you host or send the file yourself. See docs/PROTOTYPE-SHARING.md. |
| UI-35-023 | Embed prototype | partial | The dialog generates a copyable iframe snippet with sandbox="allow-scripts allow-popups allow-popups-to-escape-sandbox" and no allow-same-origin, tested playing inside a sandboxed iframe on a host page. It embeds a file you host, not a Forma-hosted prototype. |
| UI-35-024 | Prototype password | partial | Real AES-256-GCM encryption with a PBKDF2-SHA-256 key at 600,000 iterations and random salt and IV stored in the file; a canary test confirms the content is unreadable without the password. Anyone with file and password has the content, weak passwords can be guessed offline, a password cannot be recovered or revoked, and files open only from https:// or locally. |
| UI-35-025 | Prototype access permissions | retired | Retired October 31, 2026: Forma is an offline Mac app, so per-person access is out of scope; an exported prototype can use a password. |
| UI-35-026 | Mobile preview | partial | The exported player works at phone widths with touch, 44 px controls and safe areas, tested with Playwright iPhone 13 emulation in Chromium. Not tested on a physical phone or in mobile Safari, and there is no companion app. |
| UI-35-027 | Fixed-position layers | implemented | v0.2.0-dev.3: reachable authoring/playback path, undo/persistence and validation where applicable. Core/browser/native evidence and limits: docs/VALIDATION-v0.2-prototypes-workspaces.md. Reference acceptance remains separate. |
| UI-35-028 | Sticky-position layers | partial | Sticky works at root and in nested regions, including inside rotated parents in nested regions. A sticky layer that is itself rotated uses conservative box bounds. |
| UI-35-029 | Horizontal scroll regions | implemented | Root and nested horizontal regions work by wheel, drag and keyboard, with scroll passing outward, Scroll-to over the authored duration, While scrolling interactions and reset rules. Regions have no visible scrollbars; Motion preview is the separate UI-56-050. |
| UI-35-030 | Vertical scroll regions | implemented | Root and nested vertical regions work by wheel, drag and keyboard, with scroll passing outward, Scroll-to over the authored duration, While scrolling interactions and reset rules. Regions have no visible scrollbars; Motion preview is the separate UI-56-050. |
| UI-35-031 | Two-axis scroll regions | implemented | Root and nested regions on both axes work by wheel, drag and keyboard, with scroll passing outward, Scroll-to over the authored duration, While scrolling interactions and reset rules. Regions have no visible scrollbars; Motion preview is the separate UI-56-050. On both axes; unit tests cover two-axis regions and routing, and the browser spec drives vertical regions. Unit tests cover two-axis regions and routing; the browser spec drives vertical regions. |
| UI-35-032 | Scroll overflow setting | implemented | v0.2.0-dev.3: reachable authoring/playback path, undo/persistence and validation where applicable. Core/browser/native evidence and limits: docs/VALIDATION-v0.2-prototypes-workspaces.md. Reference acceptance remains separate. |
| UI-35-033 | Scroll-to animation | implemented | Scroll-to follows the action's duration and easing in the ordinary presenter and Motion preview; instant or reduced motion jumps; wheel, touch or pointer input interrupts. |
| UI-35-034 | Prototype audio playback | partial | Explicit local canonical PCM16 WAV attachment and steady-frame presentation controls: Play/Pause/seek/restart, loop/volume, single owned voice, cancellation and Undo/save. Strict byte/duration/float budgets, private transfer/static warnings and metadata-only MCP redaction are tested. No autoplay/reaction/Motion/transition/mixing/compressed-codec or native audible parity claim. See docs/PROTOTYPE-AUDIO.md. |
| UI-35-035 | Prototype video playback | partial | Existing embedded MP4/WebM prototype playback is reachable and browser-validated: transforms/clips, loop/mute/start time, restart/navigation pause and visibility audio gates. This reconciles an overlooked planned row with the already implemented media path, without claiming a new renderer. Browser/native codec and autoplay policies apply; nonrectangular clips/masks require manual Play, and remote media, trimming/transcoding and full native/reference acceptance remain unfinished. See docs/EMBEDDED-MEDIA.md and tests/e2e/media.spec.ts. |
| UI-35-036 | Presentation reduced motion | implemented | v0.2.0-dev.3: reachable authoring/playback path, undo/persistence and validation where applicable. Core/browser/native evidence and limits: docs/VALIDATION-v0.2-prototypes-workspaces.md. Reference acceptance remains separate. |

| UI-36-003 | Comment author display | implemented | File-local threads show saved authors for posts/replies. New messages use an explicit session display name; legacy absent authors stay unknown. No account authentication or author permissions. See docs/LOCAL-COMMENT-THREADS.md. |
| UI-36-004 | Comment creation timestamp | implemented | New file-local posts/replies save creation timestamps and display localized times; edits retain creation time plus edited timestamp. Legacy absent time remains unrecorded. Validation, Undo and persistence are checked. See docs/LOCAL-COMMENT-THREADS.md. |
| UI-36-005 | Comment text editing | implemented | Thread panel edits root/reply text through source-bound drafts and ordinary document history, preserving authors/creation times and recording edited time. Stale same-event actions refuse; bounded legacy text is disclosed. See docs/LOCAL-COMMENT-THREADS.md. |
| UI-36-006 | Delete own comment | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-36-007 | Reply to comment | implemented | Thread panel posts, edits and deletes bounded file-local replies with explicit local author/time, Undo/save/reopen, per-thread pagination and malformed-input refusal. No mentions, remote delivery or account ownership. See docs/LOCAL-COMMENT-THREADS.md. |
| UI-36-008 | Mention collaborator | retired | Retired October 31, 2026: Forma is an offline Mac app, so mentioning other people is out of scope. |
| UI-36-009 | Resolve comment | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-36-010 | Reopen comment | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-36-011 | Filter unresolved comments | implemented | Cross-page local thread panel filters unresolved threads with bounded indexing/search and explicit truncation diagnostics; filtering changes no document history. See docs/LOCAL-COMMENT-THREADS.md. |
| UI-36-012 | Filter resolved comments | implemented | Cross-page local thread panel filters resolved threads and reopens them through Undoable edits; filtering changes no project. See docs/LOCAL-COMMENT-THREADS.md. |
| UI-36-013 | Filter comments by author | implemented | Local thread panel filters saved root/reply author names, alongside status/search and result pagination. Legacy unknown identity is explicit; no cloud user picker. See docs/LOCAL-COMMENT-THREADS.md. |
| UI-36-014 | Jump to comment | implemented | Jump selects the actual page/anchored layer and centers the derived affine pin position using the logical SVG viewport, preserving artwork and Undo. Missing/collapsed anchors use disclosed saved-position fallback. Source/identity/context guards prevent stale retargeting. See docs/LOCAL-COMMENT-THREADS.md. |
| UI-36-015 | Comment thread panel | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-36-016 | Comment notification | retired | Retired October 31, 2026: Forma is an offline Mac app, so notifications from other people are out of scope. |
| UI-36-017 | Comment link | partial | Local thread links work. Reply-specific targets, an OS link handler and cross-file lookup are absent. |
| UI-36-018 | Emoji reaction | partial | Eight fixed emoji reactions on local threads, with no full picker. |
| UI-36-019 | Copy comment text | implemented | Copy root/reply plain text from the local thread panel; browser clipboard refusal exposes a selected read-only text fallback for standard Command-C. No fabricated native node/SVG clipboard representation or expanded IPC. See docs/LOCAL-COMMENT-THREADS.md. |
| UI-36-020 | Comment on prototype | implemented | Threads are ordinary comments with optional surface:'prototype': reply, resolve, delete, filter, link. Pins scroll with frame content; threads left in Design on the frame or its layers also appear. Lists 200 threads per frame. Older builds open these files. docs/PROTOTYPE-COMMENTS.md. |
| UI-36-021 | Comment permissions | retired | Retired October 31, 2026: Forma is an offline Mac app, so shared comment permissions are out of scope. |
| UI-36-022 | Annotation tool | implemented | Annotate on canvas opens the editor at the clicked point; Measure on canvas takes two clicks with snapping to box points. Both also reach View and Actions, with Undo and persistence. Keyboard users have percentage fields in the sidebar. |
| UI-36-023 | Annotation text | implemented | Inspect → Layer annotations creates/edits/removes literal plain-text notes with Undo/save/reopen, original-context guards, locks and component defaults/root overrides. Private metadata is excluded from artwork, library and explicit Figma delivery. See docs/LOCAL-LAYER-ANNOTATIONS.md. |
| UI-36-024 | Annotation category | implemented | Notes pick a category from their layer or from any category in the file, which copies it to the layer. A category exists while a layer uses it. Older builds refuse files using the new annotation fields. |
| UI-36-025 | Annotation categories management | implemented | File-wide rename, recolor, merge and delete in one Undo, reporting locked or inherited layers left unchanged. Empty file-level categories do not exist. |
| UI-36-026 | Annotated property reference | implemented | Twenty-one live properties, adding fill, stroke, radius, type, blend, layout and prototype target. Stacks summarize as a count; mismatched types read as unavailable. |
| UI-36-027 | Measurement annotation | implemented | Box width, height and diagonal, any two box points (optionally along one page axis), and painted width and height. Painted bounds use visualBounds, so they are conservative. |
| UI-36-028 | Distance annotation | implemented | Box centers, centers along an axis, nearest box edge gap or overlap, or two chosen points. All live; a missing target reads as unavailable. Edge gaps use transformed boxes, not contours. |
| UI-36-029 | Developer note | implemented | Inspect creates and edits file-local developer notes, filters them and displays them in cross-page handoff annotations. Validation, context guards, Undo/persistence and component root override semantics apply; no hosted developer assignment. See docs/LOCAL-LAYER-ANNOTATIONS.md. |
| UI-36-030 | Annotation visibility | implemented | Saved per-layer visibility, a global marker toggle, and a detached overlay showing text, live values and lines. It never enters exported artwork. Labels are not rearranged when they overlap. |

| UI-38-014 | Recover after crash | implemented | Bounded write-ahead journal plus transactional recovery. A real renderer kill before autosave restores pending edits as one Undo step; stale or invalid journals are discarded. Edits inside the ~60 ms throttle, patches over 2 MB and OS/machine failure can still lose work. See docs/RECOVERY-RELIABILITY.md. |

Browser tests cover migration, restoration/undo and a real embedded image larger than the previous storage quota. |

Browser tests cover migration, restoration/undo and a real embedded image larger than the previous storage quota. |
| UI-38-017 | Version author | implemented | Immutable local name and avatar labels on new named versions. |

Browser tests cover migration, restoration/undo and a real embedded image larger than the previous storage quota. |
| UI-38-019 | Version description | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-38-020 | Preview older version | implemented | File → Version history → Preview loads a validated historical snapshot lazily and shows any saved page without mutating/registering historical fonts in the editor. Shared bounded cover renderer reports unsupported/missing-font limits. No pixel-perfect reference claim. See docs/LOCAL-VERSION-REVIEW.md. |
| UI-38-021 | Restore older version | implemented | v0.2.0-dev.2: transactional IndexedDB documents/active pointer and named snapshots; additive migration preserves old localStorage copies. Browser tests cover migration, restoration/undo and a real embedded image larger than the previous storage quota. |
| UI-38-022 | Duplicate version as new file | implemented | A reviewed named version duplicates into an independent local file in All files without replacing the current design. Full validation, original-context checks and abortable repository writes prevent stale actions. Scoped internal references stay intact. See docs/LOCAL-VERSION-REVIEW.md. |
| UI-38-023 | Version link | partial | Local version links work. An OS protocol handler and foreign-file loading are absent. |
| UI-38-024 | Compare versions | implemented | Named local version → Compare with current design matches stable page/layer IDs, reports additions/removals/changes/cross-page moves and layer order changes. Object key order/timestamps do not create changes. Structural comparison only; no pixel diff or author attribution. See docs/LOCAL-VERSION-REVIEW.md. |
| UI-38-025 | Version change summary | implemented | Version comparison summarizes complete layer/page/document changes and named fields, with 200 bounded entries and explicit count/budget diagnostics. Report omits property values, comment bodies, URLs and image/font bytes. See docs/LOCAL-VERSION-REVIEW.md. |
| UI-38-026 | Branch creation | implemented | File → Branches forks atomically (main, base snapshot, working copy, registry) with a name check, 50 per file, no nesting. Local only; branches are not stored in .oui. See docs/LOCAL-BRANCHES.md. |
| UI-38-027 | Branch review | partial | Conflicts plus structural base→branch and base→main summaries with previews; "Mark as reviewed" stamps the exact branch revision with the local profile label. Single-user: no other reviewers, comments on the review or pixel diff. |
| UI-38-028 | Branch merge | implemented | Pure headless three-way merge by page and layer identity, run from main, one Undo step, full validation before commit. Text does not merge within a string; no update-branch-from-main; dangling prototype links stay as they do on delete. |
| UI-38-029 | Branch conflict resolution | implemented | Per-conflict main/branch choice for layer properties, delete vs edit, moves, cycles, stacking, pages, keyed library items and deleted component/variable/style references. Merge stays blocked until all are answered; resolutions are not persisted across dialog closes. |
| UI-38-030 | Branch archive | implemented | Archive refuses edits, Restore reopens, and only archived branches delete from the device. Permanent deletion of main cascades to branches, bases and persisted Undo. |
| UI-38-031 | Branch status | implemented | Real ahead, behind, diverged, conflict, merged and archived states from content comparison, with a top-bar indicator and native window title. Status reads each branch, so very large files are slow to fill. |
| UI-38-032 | Main branch protection | partial | Per-file toggle refuses direct edits to main and requires a review of the current branch revision to merge. Self-imposed, no permissions; Undo/Redo stay available; MCP and direct .oui writes are outside the guard. |
| UI-38-033 | Edit history persistence | implemented | Undo/Redo persist as layer patches in the same transaction as recovery. Limits: 60 steps, 12 MB total, 6 MB per step, 40 ms encoding per save; a stale or damaged record is ignored and over-quota history is dropped so the document still saves. In-memory Undo remains 100 steps; an explicit file open starts fresh. |
| UI-38-034 | File corruption recovery | partial | A stored file failing validation opens recovery choices from startup, Home or Storage: newest version as a copy, reported repaired copy, raw download; the record is never changed. Does not repair an IndexedDB database that cannot open. |
| UI-38-035 | Backup export | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-38-036 | Storage quota notification | implemented | File → Storage and recovery shows quota, durability and measured sizes; once-per-level notices at 80% and 95%; a specific "storage is full" message on failed writes; explicit durable storage request. Validated with simulated quota values and errors. |
