## Dev Mode and inspection

| ID | Required behavior | Status | Verification / limits |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| UI-41-001 | Dev Mode workspace | partial | Dev Mode toolbar button, Inspect tab and command enter one workspace with a persistent bar, handoff counts and reduced tools. Canvas drags, handles, double-click, placement, nudge, opacity keys and artwork commands refuse, while metadata panels and Undo stay available. Layers-panel visibility, lock and rename are not guarded, and read-only is an interface guard. See docs/DEV-MODE-WORKSPACE.md. |
| UI-41-002 | Layer CSS generation | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-41-003 | Copy CSS | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-41-004 | CSS units selection | implemented | Inspect px/rem controls with an explicit root pixel size; converted CSS and measurements, transient UI state. Other relative units need containing context. See docs/DEV-INSPECTION.md. |
| UI-41-005 | CSS property list | implemented | Read-only property rows, per-row/CSS/filtered-report copy, search and multi-selected-layer choice. Approximate CSS warnings; bounded metadata and copy. See docs/DEV-INSPECTION.md. |
| UI-41-006 | iOS property inspection | implemented | Adds @1x–@3x pixels, parent-relative positions, the file's color space, shadows and nearest Dynamic Type style. Adds SwiftUI and UIKit code for one layer. Type-checked once by hand; children and vector art are not generated. |
| UI-41-007 | Android property inspection | implemented | Adds mdpi–xxxhdpi pixels, nearest Material type style, shadows, and Compose and XML code for one layer. Output has not been compiled. Display P3 uses ColorSpaces.DisplayP3; XML converts to sRGB and says so. |
| UI-41-008 | Color code format | implemented | Inspect HEX/RGB/HSL formatting with alpha for supported CSS and metadata; transient selection. See docs/DEV-INSPECTION.md. |
| UI-41-009 | Layer measurements | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-41-010 | Spacing inspection | implemented | Measure offers layout boxes, painted bounds, or inside a rotated parent along its own axes. Hidden layers and non-parents refuse. Not nearest contour distances. |
| UI-41-011 | Padding inspection | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-41-012 | Gap inspection | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-41-013 | Typography inspection | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-41-014 | Applied style inspection | implemented | Names, authored values and effective base values for linked paint/text/effect/stroke styles, plus guide style and missing-link labels. See docs/DEV-INSPECTION.md. |
| UI-41-015 | Applied variable inspection | implemented | Bound field, actual variable, resolved value, effective base property, Web syntax and missing-reference retained literals. See docs/DEV-INSPECTION.md. |
| UI-41-016 | Variable mode inspection | implemented | Effective local collection modes identify layer/ancestor/document/default provenance and per-alias default fallback. See docs/DEV-INSPECTION.md. |
| UI-41-017 | Alias chain inspection | implemented | Named iterative alias traces include collection/mode/fallback, missing/cycle labels and visible 256-hop guard. See docs/DEV-INSPECTION.md. |
| UI-41-018 | Download selected asset | implemented | Layer or subtree download in SVG, PNG, JPG or saved profiles; base artwork needs Motion closed. |
| UI-41-019 | Automatic icon detection | partial | Partial: Inspect automatically compares eligible transparent vector-container geometry with all 1,951 installed licensed Lucide icons in a bounded local worker. Normalized contour comparison accepts reversal/order/start offsets within explicit tolerance, keeps ambiguous known results and never guesses semantics/provenance. Names/colors/stroke weights/root pose are excluded; private IDs/text/comments/assets never enter numeric signatures. Deadline/cancel and permanent source/local-session receipts reject stale results; artwork/Undo remain unchanged. Unsupported composites, raster/network/special-stroke geometry refuse. See docs/AUTOMATIC-ICON-GEOMETRY.md. Native/reference coverage pending. |
| UI-41-020 | Ready-for-development status | implemented | Inspect marks page or one unlocked non-managed layer ready for development, with badges/search, component defaults and explicit instance-root overrides/reset. Legacy section Ready flags remain compatible. One Undo/save/reopen; no cloud assignment or completion verification. See docs/LOCAL-HANDOFF-STATUS.md. |
| UI-41-021 | Completed development status | implemented | Inspect marks page or unlocked editable layer completed, resets to no status, filters completed layers and keeps source/instance override semantics. Validation, context/Motion guards, Undo/save/reopen apply. Local authored status, not verified code completion. See docs/LOCAL-HANDOFF-STATUS.md. |
| UI-41-022 | Handoff section list | implemented | Inspect handoff list searches ready/completed pages, sections and layers across the file; status/scope filters, bounded indexing, 50-row pagination and actual page/layer navigation preserve hidden/locked artwork. No hosted developer workspace. See docs/LOCAL-HANDOFF-STATUS.md. |
| UI-41-023 | Handoff annotation display | implemented | Inspect shows actual file-local handoff annotation text, kinds/categories and live supported values across pages, with search/type filtering, bounded results and source-safe navigation to hidden/locked owners. Source references and private metadata survive .oui; no hosted annotation backend. See docs/LOCAL-LAYER-ANNOTATIONS.md. |
| UI-41-024 | Developer links | implemented | Inspect → Developer resources attaches named file-local HTTP(S) links; explicit isolated open/copy, no automatic requests. Validated metadata, real Undo/save/reopen, locks and stale-context guards. Resource sites retain their own permissions. See docs/DEVELOPER-RESOURCES.md. |
| UI-41-025 | Attach issue link | implemented | Inspect → Developer resources → Issue accepts a named validated HTTP(S) issue URL, editable/removable/copyable with Undo/save/reopen. File-local attachment only; no issue-account authentication or status synchronization. See docs/DEVELOPER-RESOURCES.md. |
| UI-41-026 | Attach documentation link | implemented | Inspect → Developer resources → Documentation attaches a named validated HTTP(S) documentation URL with explicit open/copy, editing/removal, Undo and persistence. No remote content embedding or automatic fetch. See docs/DEVELOPER-RESOURCES.md. |
| UI-41-027 | Attach repository link | implemented | Inspect → Developer resources → Repository attaches a named validated HTTP(S) repository URL with explicit open/copy, editing/removal, Undo and persistence. No repository discovery, account access or code mapping. See docs/DEVELOPER-RESOURCES.md. |
| UI-41-028 | Component playground | partial | Partial: reachable Assets/Inspect component playground derives an isolated fresh default instance through shared component/layout/style validation. Try exact variants, exposed text/Boolean/swap values and supported ordinary nested swaps; reset without source/selection/history mutations. Static isolated SVG with bounded dependency/font/output work, source/local-generation cancellation and inherited locks. Slot-bearing, circular/oversized or unsupported ancestor/context dependencies refuse; no insertion, prototype execution, Code Connect or cloud controls. See docs/COMPONENT-PLAYGROUND.md. Focused core/browser/type/MCP and independent review; native/reference/broader coverage pending. |
| UI-41-029 | Inspect variant properties | implemented | Read-only component-set variant values/description and nearest nested-instance main context. See docs/DEV-INSPECTION.md. |
| UI-41-030 | Inspect instance overrides | implemented | Explicit root/descendant override values compared with source and component-property defaults; 2000-row budget, 200-row pages, redacted embedded assets and 1MB copy refusal. See docs/DEV-INSPECTION.md. |
| UI-41-031 | Compare design revisions | partial | Partial: File > Version history compares named local saved revisions with the current file by stable layer/page identities, value-free field summaries, complete 50-row pagination and kind/name/page/field filters. Shows separately fitted saved/current exact-page artwork, with missing-page diagnostics. Read-only, bounded, no pixel diff, synchronized viewport, branch comparison or per-edit author attribution. See docs/LOCAL-VERSION-REVIEW.md. Eleven focused core and thirteen browser checks plus editor TypeScript; independent read review clear. Native/reference acceptance pending. |
| UI-41-032 | Code Connect component mapping | partial | Inspect maps a component or component set to a code component per language with source path, property and value maps, stored in the optional validated codeConnect document array with one Undo. Hand-authored and file-local: no repository reading, CLI, publishing or library/Bridge transfer. Deleted targets show as removable orphans. See docs/CODE-CONNECT.md. |
| UI-41-033 | Code Connect snippets | partial | Generates copyable snippets from the instance's real variant and property values for TSX, JSX, Vue, Svelte, HTML, Swift and Kotlin, with optional {{prop}} templates and nested swap mappings. Text is never executed and is not compiler-checked. |
| UI-41-034 | Codegen plugin selection | partial | Inspect's Code generator list offers built-in CSS plus local plugins declaring codegen:run, remembered per device outside the file. Each Generate is one explicit sandbox run with only a redacted layer, and every other capability refuses. Forma plugin format only, with no automatic regeneration on selection. |
| UI-41-035 | VS Code integration | planned | reference-verification-pending |
| UI-41-036 | Dev resource management | implemented | Inspect manages bounded resource lists (32/layer, 4,096/document): strict URL/shape validation, attach/edit/remove/open/copy, locks, context cancellation, component defaults/overrides/reset, library-update propagation, Undo/save/reopen and reports. Bridge explicitly omits links without flattening resource-only instance overrides; no Dev Resources API/hosted synchronization claim. See docs/DEVELOPER-RESOURCES.md. |

## Plugins widgets and extensions

| ID | Required behavior | Status | Verification / limits |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| UI-43-001 | Plugin discovery | implemented | Local manager with search, favorites, recents and Actions search over installed packages. |
| UI-43-002 | Plugin installation | implemented | Reviewed install shows permissions, interface, network origins and code generation languages. Bounded local JSON packages. |
| UI-43-003 | Plugin removal | implemented | Remove an installed local command package through guarded transactional local storage. Existing files/artwork stay unchanged; cross-window catalog invalidation and reinstall paths are validated. This describes Forma-local package removal, with separate native/reference verification pending. See docs/LOCAL-COMMAND-PLUGINS.md. |
| UI-43-004 | Plugin launch | implemented | Explicit per-run launch; an interface run stays alive until close, Cancel, source change or the 10-minute limit. Frame and worker verified in a WKWebView harness; the app itself was not launched. |
| UI-43-005 | Plugin favorites | implemented | Per-device favorites with toggle, manager row and Actions ranking, surviving reload, never in .oui, cleared on package removal. Native reference pending. |
| UI-43-006 | Recent plugins | implemented | Explicit runs fill a per-device Recently run row (8 entries, clearable), surviving reload, never in .oui. Native reference pending. |
| UI-43-007 | Plugin permissions display | implemented | Installed-package review and run controls display every requested supported capability with explicit per-run grants; permissions are checked per request and before reviewed Apply. Bounded read/write capability details are visible; broader APIs remain separately planned. Native/reference verification pending. See docs/LOCAL-COMMAND-PLUGINS.md. |
| UI-43-008 | Plugin iframe UI | partial | ui HTML up to 100 KB in an opaque frame; scripts run in a terminable worker and cannot freeze the editor. Mirrored DOM limits: geometry lags a frame, preventDefault applies from the next event, no MutationObserver. Dialog-bound. |
| UI-43-009 | Plugin parameter input | partial | Strict typed inputs through trusted host controls with a copied snapshot. No dynamic parameter schemas. |
| UI-43-010 | Plugin message bridge | implemented | Carries async requests, reviewed commits and bounded interface messages, with source-window checks and budgets. Malformed or flooded traffic ends the run. |
| UI-43-011 | Plugin document read API | partial | Reads add fills, radius, typography, child IDs and a parentId filter, plus image and export reads. Comments, annotations and code source stay excluded; no styles, variables or components. |
| UI-43-012 | Plugin document write API | partial | Up to 20 reviewed forma.commit proposals per run, each one Undo step, plus a final propose. Appearance updates work on parents. No delete, reparent or effects. |
| UI-43-013 | Plugin node creation | partial | Creates rectangle, ellipse, text, image, frame, group, line, polygon and star, nested with parent-relative coordinates. No components, vectors, masks or sections. |
| UI-43-014 | Plugin selection API | implemented | Selection read with child IDs and fills, plus reviewed delivery, mid-run through commit. The dialog is modal, so there is no selection-change event. |
| UI-43-015 | Plugin font loading | implemented | fonts:read lists and loads bundled, embedded and registered families, and only loaded families can enter a proposal. Loading never downloads. |
| UI-43-016 | Plugin image API | implemented | image:write takes PNG, JPEG, GIF and WebP up to 2 MB after decode, as layers or image fills shown in review. image:read returns one layer up to 4 MB. No SVG input or video. |
| UI-43-017 | Plugin export API | partial | export:read renders one layer to SVG, PNG or JPG, up to 4 MB, scale 0.1–4. No PDF or batch. |
| UI-43-018 | Plugin storage | implemented | storage:local get/set/delete/keys in a separate per-plugin IndexedDB record (200 keys, 256 KB), persisting across runs and reload, isolated per plugin, never in .oui, cleared on Remove. Native WKWebView not exercised. |
| UI-43-019 | Plugin networking permissions | partial | No network by default. Requires manifest allowedOrigins (exact https or loopback origins), reviewed display and a per-run network:fetch grant, enforced in the host and again by a separate frame that narrows its own connect-src; tests show zero requests to unlisted origins. Target must allow Origin: null; text responses to 1 MB; 25 requests per run; tested against loopback servers only. |
| UI-43-020 | Plugin error isolation | implemented | Worker, bridge and interface failures, including hangs, remove owned frames with no proposal applied. Not an OS or memory boundary. |
| UI-43-021 | Plugin cancellation | implemented | Cancel, invalidation, deadline and watchdog remove interface and network frames. Stopping a spinning script verified in real WKWebView. |
| UI-43-022 | Plugin development manifest | implemented | Closed manifest with optional ui, network, codegen and 11 permissions, each section paired with its permission. |
| UI-43-023 | Plugin reload | partial | Reload installed local catalog or replace a reviewed package; explicit rerun creates a fresh worker and invalidates old grants/handlers. No automatic code/file watching or Figma development runtime. See docs/LOCAL-COMMAND-PLUGINS.md. |
| UI-43-024 | Plugin console | implemented | Bounded console for plugin logs, interface console output and errors, and each network request. |
| UI-43-025 | Widget insertion | implemented | Import, review, install and insert from the manager, whiteboard toolbar or Actions. Install never runs code. |
| UI-43-026 | Widget state synchronization | implemented | Validated JSON (32 KB) on the frame, in step with its layers through Undo, duplicate and save. Stale results are refused. |
| UI-43-027 | Widget resizing | partial | Canvas handles and size fields resize within package limits, and host layout regenerates the layers from the saved view. Guest code gets the new size on its next event, not on each resize, and text layout is estimated. |
| UI-43-028 | Widget interaction | partial | Host hit targets send an action name to a fresh worker, one Undo step per event. Adds host-owned text input. No hover or timers; rotated widgets use the bounding box. |
| UI-43-029 | Widget property menu | implemented | Host panel with action, toggle, choice, color and separator items (at most 12), plus refresh, console and convert to layers. |
| UI-43-030 | Widget permissions | implemented | selection:read and page:read, granted per package, session-only and revocable. Widgets change only their own layers. |
| UI-43-031 | Shader discovery and filtering | implemented | Local browser with search, kind and tag filters. This is the bundled catalog and file shaders only: no community or hosted library. |
| UI-43-032 | Shader source filtering | partial | Filters between Forma bundled and This file. No team, community or account sources exist. |
| UI-43-033 | Shader code inspection | implemented | Read-only source, per-version view, uniform table and shared prelude. File shaders can also be edited, with compiler errors shown and bad source refused. |
| UI-43-034 | Generate shader fill with agent | retired | Retired October 31, 2026: Forma is an offline Mac app, so AI generation is out of scope. |
| UI-43-035 | Generate shader effect with agent | retired | Retired October 31, 2026: Forma is an offline Mac app, so AI generation is out of scope. |
| UI-43-036 | Edit shader with agent | retired | Retired October 31, 2026: Forma is an offline Mac app, so AI generation is out of scope. |
| UI-43-037 | Generative plugin creation with agent | retired | Retired October 31, 2026: Forma is an offline Mac app, so AI generation is out of scope. |
| UI-43-038 | Generative plugin prompt editing | retired | Retired October 31, 2026: Forma is an offline Mac app, so AI generation is out of scope. |

## Accessibility and localization

| ID | Required behavior | Status | Verification / limits |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| UI-44-001 | Accessible button names | implemented | A Chromium accessibility-tree audit found no unnamed buttons across chrome, Home, four inspector layer types, Slides, Whiteboard and 36 dialog states. Native VoiceOver acceptance pending. |
| UI-44-002 | Accessible tool names | implemented | Toolbar tools expose names and aria-pressed, and option menus and the audit cover them. Native VoiceOver acceptance pending. |
| UI-44-003 | Accessible menu items | implemented | Web menu bar has a named menubar, named menu per group, aria-haspopup, and ARIA menu bar keys, validated by keyboard. The native NSMenu is outside this path. |
| UI-44-004 | Accessible form labels | implemented | The same audit found no unnamed text, number, select, checkbox, slider or color controls in the audited surfaces. Native acceptance pending. |
| UI-44-005 | Accessible selection announcements | implemented | Polite debounced page/selection/state/geometry live announcements for the shared Design/Whiteboard editor; DOM/browser validated, native VoiceOver acceptance pending. See docs/DOCUMENT-ACCESSIBILITY.md. |
| UI-44-006 | Screen-reader document outline | implemented | Accessible searchable hierarchy outline with cross-page keyboard navigation, inherited states, 100-row rendering and read-only selection. Native VoiceOver acceptance pending. See docs/DOCUMENT-ACCESSIBILITY.md. |
| UI-44-007 | Keyboard-only layer creation | partial | Guarded keyboard insertion dialog for ten basic layer types, bounded geometry, Undo/save; freehand/pen/media and workspace-specific objects remain unfinished. See docs/DOCUMENT-ACCESSIBILITY.md. |
| UI-44-008 | Keyboard-only selection | implemented | Keyboard outline navigation/selection complements Layers and canvas parent/child/sibling paths, cross-page selection and hidden/locked inspection without mutation. See docs/DOCUMENT-ACCESSIBILITY.md. |
| UI-44-009 | Keyboard-only transform | implemented | Keyboard numerical move/resize/rotation fields and canvas nudge paths validated with Undo/save and inherited-lock refusal. See docs/DOCUMENT-ACCESSIBILITY.md. |
| UI-44-010 | Keyboard-only property editing | partial | Keyboard-only edits of position, size (with arithmetic), hex fill and the color slider are validated with Undo. Canvas-only gestures such as gradient handles have not been audited for numeric equivalents on every property. |
| UI-44-011 | Keyboard-only export | implemented | Export shortcut, Tab and Enter produce a real browser download. The native save panel is not validated. |
| UI-44-012 | Visible focus indicators | implemented | One token gives a 2px ring (3px in Increased contrast) at 3:1 or better in both themes, checked at every Tab stop in the chrome and 36 dialog states. Text fields now get an outline, not only a 1px border change. |
| UI-44-013 | Logical focus order | implemented | Tab order is header, menu bar, layers, canvas; F6 leaves the canvas, where Tab selects siblings, then toolbar and properties. Each layer row's lock and hide buttons remain separate Tab stops, so long documents have long sidebars. |
| UI-44-014 | Focus trapping in dialogs | implemented | One app-wide manager gives every aria-modal dialog initial focus, Tab containment, stray-focus return and opener return, guarded by document id. Validated across 36 dialog states. |
| UI-44-015 | Escape closes dialogs | implemented | Escape closed all 36 dialog states tested, and all 29 modal components now handle it. I added the missing handler to PrototypeRenderBoundary. |
| UI-44-016 | Contrast compliant controls | partial | Text, accent and focus tokens pass 4.5:1 and 3:1 in both themes, and rendered text passes in the editor, menus, four dialogs and Home. Default --muted was darkened/lightened to reach this. Control boundaries meet 3:1 only in Increased contrast; hover states and other dialogs are unmeasured. |
| UI-44-017 | Larger UI scaling | partial | Partial: existing local Interface scale preference reaches 125% with persisted 85/100/110/125% choices, scoped chrome layout and canvas-coordinate correction across tested interactions/short windows. Artwork/presentation/export scale stays separate; no unrestricted OS text sizing or complete assistive-technology acceptance. This reconciles an already implemented and browser-tested path, not new scaling code. See docs/PREFERENCES.md and UI-45-004. Native/reference acceptance pending. |
| UI-44-018 | Reduced motion mode | partial | Partial: existing Follow system / Reduce / Allow interface-motion setting persists locally, responds to OS/storage changes and scopes chrome effects/scrolling. Editor Motion pauses when reduction activates; explicit Play remains authored preview without loop. Prototype/Slides system/file playback rules and exports remain separate. This reconciles existing validated behavior, not new motion code. See docs/INTERFACE-MOTION.md and UI-02-023. Broader/native/reference acceptance pending. |
| UI-44-019 | Color-independent selection indicators | implemented | Active tools, layer rows, pages, open menus, tabs and pressed toggles change shape as well as color, with aria-pressed or aria-current, validated by computed-style comparison. Canvas selection keeps its outlines and handles. |
| UI-44-020 | High-contrast selection | partial | Partial: persistent local black/white layer/hover/box selection outlines, resize/rotation handles and vector anchor activity on Design/Whiteboard canvas; screen-space SVG pixel, keyboard, persistence and no-artwork-mutation checks. Specialized editing overlays, Slides/presentation and a system-wide contrast theme remain outside this option. See docs/SELECTION-CONTRAST.md. |
| UI-44-021 | Accessible tooltips | implemented | Titled controls show a role=tooltip on keyboard focus, linked by aria-describedby; Escape dismisses it without moving focus. Pointer hover still uses the native title tooltip, and touch has no equivalent. |
| UI-44-022 | Accessible error announcements | partial | notifyError now speaks through one assertive live region, and inline errors already use role=alert. Failures passed directly to the toast store stay polite, because toasts have no error type. |
| UI-44-023 | Accessible comment threads | implemented | Canvas pins have keyboard activation and original-source proofs; thread dialog supports focus trap/return, focused-thread headings, labeled filters/forms, pagination, readable authors/times, errors/status and plain-copy fallback at 125% UI scale. Native assistive-technology/reference acceptance pending. See docs/LOCAL-COMMENT-THREADS.md. |
| UI-44-024 | English interface | implemented | English is the default and the source catalog, with 785 messages; t() returns the source string byte for byte. Text is unchanged except that Home file counts now use grouped digits at 1,000 or more. |
| UI-44-025 | Romanian interface | retired | Retired October 31, 2026: a Romanian interface is not needed. The existing catalog stays as an optional preference and gets no further work; English is the default. Measured interface coverage stays at 446 of 2,529 literal occurrences (17.6%), with no native-speaker review. |
| UI-44-026 | Additional language packs | partial | Language packs are one catalog file plus one registry row. One additional pack ships, a labeled Arabic preview covering menus, tools and main chrome. No user-installable packs and no native review. |
| UI-44-027 | Localized menu text | retired | Retired October 31, 2026: translated menus are not needed. The web menu bar keeps its existing Romanian text as an optional preference; the native Mac NSMenu and context menus stay English. |
| UI-44-028 | Localized dates | implemented | All 5 user-visible date sites use Intl through formatDateTime, in the language's convention, with the device region kept for English. Invalid dates show a localized fallback. |
| UI-44-029 | Locale number formatting | partial | Counts and percentages use Intl, and NumberField shows and accepts the decimal comma, including in arithmetic. Raw inspector readouts and dimension badges are not localized. |
| UI-44-030 | Right-to-left UI layout | partial | Arabic sets dir=rtl and the main chrome mirrors, including menus, toolbar arrows and sidebar resizing. Canvas geometry matches English exactly. Slides, Whiteboard, Motion and prototype panel styles are not audited. |

| UI-47-017 | Template gallery | implemented | Home and Resources/Actions expose a searchable category gallery of six original free Design, Whiteboard and Presentation templates plus bounded personal local kits. No third-party template marketplace claim. See docs/LOCAL-TEMPLATE-GALLERY.md. |
| UI-47-018 | Template preview | implemented | Selected-template page previews render isolated saved base artwork without changing the current design or registering template fonts. Existing 500-layer/4 MB font-safe preview limits and explicit diagnostics apply. See docs/LOCAL-TEMPLATE-GALLERY.md. |
| UI-47-019 | Template insertion | implemented | One selected page inserts editable artwork and supported remapped dependencies at the matching Design/Whiteboard page root with one Undo and real save/reopen. | UI-47-021 | Icon library browsing | implemented | Resources/Actions opens a licensed offline catalog of 1,951 Lucide icons with multiword search, pagination, selected preview and license display. Validated configurable size/color/stroke/position insertion creates ordinary editable vectors with one Undo and oui save/reopen in Design/Whiteboard. This is local library browsing, without an external community marketplace or slide insertion. See docs/LOCAL-ICON-LIBRARY.md. |
| UI-47-022 | Illustration library browsing | implemented | Eight original vector illustrations, searchable with previews, insert as editable vectors in one Undo. The set is small and fixed. |
| UI-47-023 | Education resources | partial | Six original offline guides, readable in the app, some linking to a tutorial. English only, and a small set. |
| UI-47-024 | Tutorial files | implemented | Three original tutorials open as real .oui files with working prototype interactions, variables, conditions and variant changes, exercised headless and with one tutorial played in the browser. Steps are not tracked or checked. English only. |
| UI-47-025 | In-app onboarding | partial | Adds the first-run offer to existing opt-in guidance. Other limits unchanged. |
| UI-47-026 | First-run canvas tour | implemented | Empty library gets an inline menu-bar offer once, with a persisted receipt. It does not auto-start, take focus or cover the canvas. Existing libraries are never offered it. Hidden below 1000 px, and clearing storage with no projects re-offers it. |
| UI-47-027 | Tool contextual help | partial | Current-tool guidance covers 17 actual toolbar tools, shows live personalized command bindings and links to keyboard shortcuts without activating tools. Specialized tools retain their own visible limits; broader contextual/reference coverage remains pending. See docs/EDITOR-LEARNING.md. |
| UI-47-028 | Help center links | implemented | Fixed trusted links open existing Forma guide, product and privacy pages through normal browser links with no project identifiers. Link destinations and real UI paths are validated; the guide is explicitly a preview that may lag source. Native/reference verification remains separate. See docs/EDITOR-LEARNING.md. |
| UI-47-029 | Release notes panel | implemented | v0.2.0-dev.9: Help/Actions opens bounded bundled release history, app-version marker, source/preview distinction, scope notes and validated public preview download/checksum. Development entries have no installer link; error/Retry/focus and unchanged selection/tool/project/history tested. Snapshot history, not live update discovery/native updater. docs/RELEASE-NOTES.md. |

## Figma Sites publishing

| ID | Required behavior | Status | Verification / limits |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| UI-50-001 | Create site project | implemented | File → New site and a Home starter create a .oui with workspace:'site'. Mac uses the existing open-document path, so no native IPC changed. Site data is additive and validated on load and on every edit. |
| UI-50-002 | Site page tree | implemented | Page tree with add, child, duplicate, delete, reorder, nest and unnest through Undo. Slugs set output folders, are validated per level, and the home page writes at the site root. |
| UI-50-003 | Site navigation links | implemented | Per-layer links to a page, web address, section or collection item, as real <a> elements with relative paths and aria-current. The Navigation block inserts links once and does not update when pages change. |
| UI-50-004 | Responsive breakpoint editor | implemented | Settings adds up to 4 custom breakpoints, and each breakpoint is a real editable frame beside Desktop. Dragging a frame's side changes its width, and invalid widths are restored. |
| UI-50-005 | Desktop breakpoint | implemented | Desktop is the page design width and resizes page roots, and the canvas edits it directly. |
| UI-50-006 | Tablet breakpoint | implemented | Tablet shows as a real canvas frame with auto layout and constraints. Edits become Tablet overrides, one Undo step each; Desktop is untouched. |
| UI-50-007 | Mobile breakpoint | implemented | Mobile works as Tablet and inherits every wider breakpoint. Deleting a layer on the frame hides it there, and new layers join the Desktop tree. |
| UI-50-008 | Breakpoint property overrides | implemented | Text, strokes, effects and gradient fills now vary per breakpoint, with the existing typed properties. Differing text ships each version and CSS shows one. Reordering and renames on a frame are not kept. |
| UI-50-009 | Responsive stack | implemented | Auto layout maps to flexbox with direction, gap, padding, sizing and wrap per breakpoint. Includes a Stack block and Stack on mobile. Desktop geometry was checked against the solver. First-on-top stacking order is not exported. |
| UI-50-010 | Responsive grid | implemented | Grid maps to CSS grid with per-breakpoint columns. Explicit placements come from placeGrid, and the Grid block defaults to 3/2/1 columns. fr tracks ignore a maximum size. |
| UI-50-011 | Site interactions | partial | Click toggles, shows or hides another layer, with keyboard and aria-expanded, plus links. No conditional logic or custom actions. |
| UI-50-012 | Hover effects | implemented | Hover scale, opacity, move, fill and text color with duration, also applied on keyboard focus. Runs in the exported site. |
| UI-50-013 | Scroll effects | implemented | Fade, slide-up and zoom reveals, parallax, and keyframed scroll timelines with start and end. Reduced motion respected. Motion does not vary per breakpoint. |
| UI-50-014 | Site animations | implemented | Five presets or custom keyframes (up to 16, opacity, position, scale, rotation) with duration, delay, loop and easing, exported as CSS @keyframes. |
| UI-50-015 | CMS collection | implemented | Local collections saved in the project, with seven validated field types. Limits are 50 collections, 50 fields and 2,000 items each. |
| UI-50-016 | CMS item editor | implemented | Item editor with typed inputs, slug, draft state, reorder, duplicate and delete through Undo. Image values are decoded before acceptance. No bulk or CSV import. |
| UI-50-017 | CMS binding | partial | Lists with limit, sort and up to 16 filters; text, image and rich text bindings; one page per item. Rich text is stored as plain source and escaped on render. No references between collections. |
| UI-50-018 | Published preview | implemented | In-app preview of the export output at each breakpoint in an opaque, CSP-pinned sandbox, with page navigation. Embeds show a note and forms never send. |
| UI-50-019 | Staging preview | retired | Retired October 31, 2026: Forma is an offline Mac app, so hosted staging is out of scope; Sites export static files. |
| UI-50-020 | Publish website | retired | Retired October 31, 2026: Forma is an offline Mac app, so hosted publishing is out of scope; Sites export static files. |
| UI-50-021 | Unpublish website | retired | Retired October 31, 2026: Forma is an offline Mac app, so hosted publishing is out of scope; Sites export static files. |
| UI-50-022 | Custom domain | retired | Retired October 31, 2026: Forma is an offline Mac app, so hosted domains are out of scope; Sites export static files. |
| UI-50-023 | Site title metadata | implemented | Site and per-page titles, plus item titles on collection pages, written to <title> and Open Graph. |
| UI-50-024 | Site description metadata | implemented | Site and per-page descriptions, plus item summaries, written to meta and Open Graph tags. |
| UI-50-025 | Open Graph image | implemented | Site and per-page images, plus an item image field, exported as files. An absolute address needs the site URL, and the export warns if it is missing. No preview card. |
| UI-50-026 | Favicon | implemented | PNG, ICO or SVG up to 512 KB with signature checks. PNG and ICO are also checked by a browser decode. |
| UI-50-027 | Site accessibility settings | implemented | Language, skip link, reduced motion and focus color, plus heading and landmark tags, alternative text, one main, and export warnings. No contrast or keyboard-order testing. |
| UI-50-028 | Site form | partial | Labelled controls with client validation, editable from the panel. Without an https endpoint, visitors are told nothing was sent. The endpoint path was not exercised end to end. |
| UI-50-029 | Site embed | partial | https address or HTML in a sandboxed iframe. YouTube, Vimeo, Spotify and Loom share links become player addresses; Forma never contacts them. Preview shows a note instead of loading embeds. |
| UI-50-030 | Site analytics | retired | Retired October 31, 2026: Forma is an offline Mac app, so hosted analytics are out of scope. |

## Figma Buzz assets

| ID | Required behavior | Status | Verification / limits |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| UI-51-001 | Create branded asset project | implemented | File → New brand project and a Files screen tile create a local project with Assets and Templates pages; the template gallery adds a Campaign starter. Stored as optional .oui data; older builds open it as a Design file without enforcing locks. |
| UI-51-002 | Brand template gallery | implemented | Search and category filter over project templates, plus a starter with six published templates in five categories. Other projects get templates through a package file. |
| UI-51-003 | Template locking | implemented | The store and MCP refuse changes to locked layers, including paste, reorder, grouping and delete; published template frames are frozen until unpublished. Files edited outside Forma are flagged by lock digest, not refused, and pasted copies of an asset become plain layers. |
| UI-51-004 | Editable content slots | implemented | Text slots allow content only, with optional brand color, brand font and character limit, enforced on panel, canvas and MCP paths. Slots freeze while In review or Approved. |
| UI-51-005 | Editable image slots | implemented | Image slots accept replacement embedded PNG, JPEG, WebP or GIF images through the panel and refuse a change to a different fill type. Replacing resets the crop. |
| UI-51-006 | Brand color constraints | implemented | Refuse or Flag mode covers fills, strokes, gradient stops, shadows and text range colors on the Assets page, with a Publish gate on templates. Draft templates are flagged only; image pixels and other pages are not checked. |
| UI-51-007 | Brand font constraints | implemented | Refuse or Flag mode matches font family names on the Assets page, including text ranges, with a Publish gate. Names only; fallback fonts are not checked and no font files are bundled. |
| UI-51-008 | Bulk text replacement | implemented | Literal find and replace, or set a slot, across checked or all assets as one Undo step. Frozen and over-length results are skipped and reported. |
| UI-51-009 | Bulk image replacement | implemented | One image for all targets, or exactly one per editable target in list order, as one Undo step. Frozen assets are skipped; a change past the 50 MB project limit is refused. |
| UI-51-010 | Asset spreadsheet input | implemented | Strict CSV and TSV by paste or file, up to 5 MB, 500 rows and 64 columns, with a column mapping and per-row review before any write. No .xlsx or live sheet connection; image cells name attached local files and web addresses are never fetched. |
| UI-51-011 | Social size presets | implemented | Eight reference size presets as batch sizes or per-asset size variations with exact frame dimensions. Layout is a proportional fit, not smart reflow; variations need the current published revision. |
| UI-51-012 | Marketing batch variations | implemented | Rows × sizes produce real locked, editable assets in one edit, so one Undo removes the batch. Batches are filterable and export normally. |
| UI-51-013 | Asset approval workflow | implemented | Status flow Draft, In review, Changes needed, Approved, with timestamps. The store enforces the slot freeze. |
| UI-51-014 | Asset version history | implemented | Compares against named versions and also keeps the last 12 automatic snapshots per asset, restorable with Undo. Images over 256 KB are not kept in snapshots. |
| UI-51-015 | Bulk asset export | implemented | One ZIP with manifest and checksums through the shared delivery pipeline: PNG, JPG or SVG at 1x to 3x, optionally approved only. Limits are 200 assets and 100 MB per archive. |
| UI-51-016 | Campaign asset folders | implemented | Projects sit in local folders, and each asset has an optional campaign that filters and searches the list. |
| UI-51-017 | Shared brand library | implemented | .formabrand.json carries the kit and published templates, with strict validation, review and additive merge. Starter kits and local libraries also work. |
| UI-51-018 | Template duplication | implemented | Duplicates a template as an independent draft with remapped slots, with Undo. |
| UI-51-019 | Template publishing | implemented | Publish gate, revisions and frozen published frames, with published templates included in the package. |
| UI-51-020 | Brand asset search | implemented | Project search covers asset and template names, category, status, size, batch, text slot content and kit values; the Files screen also matches this content. Image contents are not searched. |

## Performance and reliability

| ID | Required behavior | Status | Verification / limits |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| UI-53-001 | Large document loading | partial | Measured benchmark added and dense pages mount in batches; 20,000 layers first frame ~1.4 s vs ~5.8 s single pass (dev server, Chromium, M3 Max). The whole document is still parsed and kept in memory. See docs/PERFORMANCE-BENCHMARK.md. |
| UI-53-002 | Progressive page loading | partial | Pages with 3,000+ in-view layers mount root subtrees in ~800-layer batches with a progress indicator; pixels match the single-pass scene. Not applied to one dominant root, masks/blur fallbacks, drafts or non-Design views; later edits re-render in one pass. |
| UI-53-003 | Viewport culling | partial | Partial: bounded conservative whole-root-subtree culling on the Design/Whiteboard canvas, with opt-out, selected/editing retention, exact immutable source index and measured viewport. Real omitted DOM/defs plus on/off pixels, pan/zoom/resize/Undo/export checks. Uncertain text/effect/media/Boolean/complex-stroke roots stay rendered; root mask runs and unsupported scenes fall back fully. Shared Artwork/model/exports unchanged. See docs/CANVAS-VIEWPORT-CULLING.md. |
| UI-53-004 | Large layer tree virtualization | implemented | Cached iterative hierarchy indexing and a bounded virtualized Layers tree support logical ARIA siblings/depth, keyboard offscreen reveal, selection/filter preservation, drag/reparent, Undo/save and source-bound focus. Actual 20,000-layer flat hidden-layer files keep rendered rows bounded. Whole-canvas deep hierarchy acceptance remains separate. See docs/LAYER-TREE-VIRTUALIZATION.md. |
| UI-53-005 | Image decode scheduling | planned | reference-verification-pending |
| UI-53-006 | Font loading status | implemented | Unreleased source: Shared live task status covers catalog, bundled/Google/cache loading, embedded/imported registration, ready/failure/unavailable/superseded outcomes and retry. Compact unique-family indicator and last40 volatile task details; no invented byte percentage. Async Apply/Replace retain exact invalidatable source context, valid Undo/save and no newer-dialog close. docs/FONT-LOADING-CONTEXT.md. |
| UI-53-007 | Asset cache management | partial | Storage dialog measures embedded assets, versions, branches and Undo history and clears Undo history of closed files. Font cache behavior is unchanged; there is no separate image cache to purge, embedded assets are only reported. |
| UI-53-008 | Document write atomicity | implemented | v0.2.0-dev.2: reachable UI, version-two project persistence, validation and undo; core/browser checks in docs/VALIDATION-v0.2-systems-motion.md. Reference acceptance remains separately pending. Recovery writes document and active reference in one IndexedDB transaction. Explicit native saves remain atomic. |
| UI-53-009 | Import cancellation | partial | Terminable workers now stop .fig conversion and REST conversion immediately, as well as decoding, Sketch and PDF work. Synchronous .oui, SVG and final validation stages and native dialogs remain outside cancellation. |
| UI-53-010 | Long operation progress | partial | Imports add a converting stage with layers converted, alongside byte, stage, page and animation export progress. Merge and status checks on large files still have no progress or cancellation. |
| UI-53-011 | Corrupt import reporting | partial | Fixed .fig conversion limits and structural faults are now reported by name through the exact allowlist; other parser text stays hidden. Unsupported formats still get a report only, and repair stays .oui only. |
| UI-53-012 | Unsupported version reporting | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-53-013 | Document schema migration | implemented | v0.2: validates version 1/2, migrates v1 to v2, rejects unsupported versions; core and bridge mock coverage. |
| UI-53-014 | Duplicate ID detection | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-53-015 | Cycle detection in hierarchy | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |

| UI-53-021 | Autosave failure reporting | implemented | v0.2.0-dev.2: reachable UI, version-two project persistence, validation and undo; core/browser checks in docs/VALIDATION-v0.2-systems-motion.md. Reference acceptance remains separately pending. Recovery writes document and active reference in one IndexedDB transaction. Explicit native saves remain atomic. |
| UI-53-022 | Recoverable operation failure | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-53-023 | Renderer crash recovery | implemented | Mac: a dead WebContent process reloads the same window into its document (real-kill smoke); more than three exits in 60 s pause on Try Again. Render exceptions offer Reload, Safe mode and Save backup. A browser tab needs a user reload; journal replay is not validated inside WKWebView. |
| UI-53-024 | Safe mode startup | implemented | Two starts ended by a real WebContent SIGKILL enter safe mode. Restart normally returns to the same document and file binding, after fixing a shell session bug. Preferences are not reset. |
| UI-53-025 | Offline resource availability | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-53-026 | Missing asset placeholders | partial | Missing or unreadable .fig images now open as marked, replaceable image placeholders instead of refusing the file, and GIF and WebP fills are accepted. Missing Sketch symbols and unknown layers become visible gray placeholders. Font, video and general resource diagnostics remain separate. |
| UI-53-027 | Undo history memory bound | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-53-028 | Background save scheduling | implemented | v0.2.0-dev.2: reachable UI, version-two project persistence, validation and undo; core/browser checks in docs/VALIDATION-v0.2-systems-motion.md. Reference acceptance remains separately pending. Recovery writes document and active reference in one IndexedDB transaction. Explicit native saves remain atomic. |
| UI-53-029 | Multi-window file conflict detection | implemented | Web Locks presence, BroadcastChannel save notices and compare-and-write inside the save transaction; a stale window never overwrites newer work, and the guard holds without either API. Load newer, keep as copy, or overwrite; no merge or live sync. Conflict flow validated in browser only; the Mac app still focuses duplicates. |
| UI-53-030 | Consistent document serialization | implemented | v0.2.0-dev.2: reachable UI, version-two project persistence, validation and undo; core/browser checks in docs/VALIDATION-v0.2-systems-motion.md. Reference acceptance remains separately pending. Recovery writes document and active reference in one IndexedDB transaction. Explicit native saves remain atomic. |

It is a forecast, not a real Figma desktop plugin round trip; PDF and font substitution are not modeled. |
| UI-54-006 | Offline-first project library | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |

| UI-54-009 | No-account editing workflow | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-54-010 | Design health report | implemented | Resources → Design health report aggregates seven headless audits with layer navigation, undoable fixes and Markdown/JSON export. Results are rule counts, not a quality score; Presentations are read only and fixes are refused while Motion is open. |
| UI-54-011 | Accessibility contrast audit | partial | Automatic per-text-layer WCAG audit over painter order, with opacity compositing, worst gradient stop, worst text range and a one-Undo lightness fix. Images are not sampled: image, video, blend, partial-overlap, rotated, non-rectangular and theme-canvas backgrounds report "not evaluated". Non-text contrast and text covered from above are not assessed. |
| UI-54-012 | Missing font audit | implemented | v0.2.0-dev.3: reachable authoring/playback path, undo/persistence and validation where applicable. Core/browser/native evidence and limits: docs/VALIDATION-v0.2-prototypes-workspaces.md. Reference acceptance remains separate. Family audit includes character-range font families. |
| UI-54-013 | Unused style audit | implemented | Lists paint, text, effect, stroke and guide styles from the conservative asset index, and removal re-proves each is unreferenced with one Undo. Incomplete scans and installed-library styles are never removable; external library consumers are not visible. |
| UI-54-014 | Detached instance audit | implemented | Detach now records additive, validated detachedFrom; findings cover recorded detaches, deleted mains, orphans and likely legacy matches (name plus exact ordered type tree). Restore keeps the root ID and paint order with one Undo, replaces local edits, and is refused when prototype links, Motion tracks or comments point inside the frame. Legacy copies that were renamed or edited are not found. |
| UI-54-015 | Duplicate color audit | implemented | Indexes fills, strokes, gradient stops, text ranges, variables, paint styles and palette, with exact and ΔE 3 near groups. Merges into a new or existing variable, a style or a literal with one Undo; locked and managed layers are skipped, and paint styles link only single opaque fills. Near comparison caps at the 2,000 most used colors, and token values use the default mode. |
| UI-54-016 | Token consistency audit | implemented | Compares fill, stroke, radius, gap, padding, font size, opacity and stroke width to variables in each layer's effective mode with scopes, plus paint, text and effect style equality proved by scratch application. Finds broken and wrong-kind links; bind, snap and remove-link fixes use one Undo and skip stale rows. Instance copies are not listed, and coverage counts only these fields. |
| UI-54-017 | Batch rename preview | implemented | v0.2.0-dev.5: reachable controls with core/browser validation, undo/persistence where applicable, universal Mac build and native smoke. See docs/VALIDATION-v0.2-controls-workflows.md. Exact Figma reference acceptance remains separate. Batch rename shows every resulting name and validates the complete selection before a single edit. |
| UI-54-018 | Multi-format export queue | implemented | File → Export queue holds snapshot rows in format sets or custom SVG/PNG/JPG, with sequential render, failure isolation, cancel, retry and a ZIP manifest listing what is missing. Session-only, 200 files and 100 MB; PDF stays in Export. |
| UI-54-019 | Document minimap navigation | implemented | View/Actions → Show overview minimap shows current-page schematic bounds, camera viewport, click/drag/keyboard navigation and Fit/F, with camera history and no saved-artwork edit. Cached bounded affine transforms, logical viewport/scaled tracks and permanent source/gesture interruption proofs are tested. Approximate clips, no masks/effect ink or all-pages atlas. See docs/DOCUMENT-MINIMAP.md. |
| UI-54-020 | Command usage learning | implemented | Help → Command usage is opt-in and stores only per-command counts on the device, feeding a Frequently used section in Actions and shortcut tips. Actions that bypass commands, such as arrow nudges and tool keys, are not counted. |
| UI-54-021 | Custom workspace layouts | implemented | View → Workspace layouts saves up to 24 named arrangements of sidebar widths and collapse, left tab, rulers, grid, outlines, minimap and scrollbars, applied live and never touching a document. Forma has no dockable panels, so panels cannot be rearranged; layouts cannot be shared or bound to shortcuts. |
| UI-54-022 | Side-by-side device preview | implemented | View → Side-by-side device preview reflows one Design frame at up to six device or custom sizes through the shared resize derivation, with per-size overflow counts and per-card refusal, and never touches the document. Previews are static images: prototypes do not run, and the 16 MB resize limit applies. |
| UI-54-023 | Design-to-code export presets | partial | File → Design-to-code export generates HTML/CSS, React, SwiftUI and Compose plus five token formats, with seven built-in presets and saved presets. Output is approximate with listed reductions: images and vectors are placeholders, and gradients, effects and mixed text are reduced. Generated native code is not compiled and output is not visually compared. |
| UI-54-024 | Cross-tool color palette exchange | partial | Resources → palette exchange reads and writes .ase, .aco, .gpl, .sketchpalette, .swatches, design tokens JSON, CSS and hex lists, and also reads Tailwind-shaped JSON, with bounded validation and one-Undo import. Solid opaque colors only; CMYK and LAB convert without a profile. Tested with its own writers and one hand-built .ase, not files from the real applications. |
| UI-54-025 | Reusable personal starter kits | implemented | Bounded private device-local full project starter kits support named save, lazy preview, independent creation/export, removal Undo and persistence without community publishing or cloud sync. Strict envelopes, transactional byte/count limits and source-bound abort protect existing data. See docs/LOCAL-TEMPLATE-GALLERY.md. |
| UI-54-026 | One-click bug report bundle | implemented | Help → Create bug report bundle saves a local ZIP of counts, safe settings and redacted errors, and a canary test proves no document strings reach it. Including the document is an unchecked per-bundle opt-in that resets after each save and adds a separately named file. Forma never transmits it; toast messages are not captured, and native save was not exercised. |
| UI-54-027 | Local automation API | partial | mcp/automation.ts is a source-checkout JSON CLI using the same transactions, validators and locks as MCP. No installed binary, stable API promise or in-app scripting host. |
| UI-54-028 | Scriptable batch transforms | implemented | Object → Batch transforms runs validated declarative JSON scripts (scope, filters, seven operations) with a private-copy preview and one Undo; they can be saved or exported. Nothing is evaluated, there are no conditionals or variables, and locked or managed layers are skipped. JavaScript automation stays in Local plugins. |
| UI-54-029 | Privacy dashboard | implemented | Help → Privacy dashboard shows live local stores, counts records without reading values, reads the enforced connect-src policy and observed hosts, and clears selected stores after confirmation. It discloses the Google Fonts request, which cannot be turned off, and plugin requests to origins you grant per run. Projects, fonts and plugins are managed from their own screens. |
| UI-54-030 | Windows portability layer | planned | reference-verification-pending |

| UI-56-052 | Export animation MP4 | implemented | MP4 encodes frame by frame through WebCodecs as H.264 (High realtime, then Baseline) with exact timestamps and frame count, a stall watchdog, and optional AAC from a frame's audio or a picked WAV. Verified through the UI with ffprobe in Chromium and Playwright WebKit, and at encoder level in system WKWebView; the packaged app and older macOS were not run. Embedded video layers export their poster, and MediaRecorder remains the fallback. docs/ANIMATION-VIDEO-EXPORT.md. The real app also records, saves and decodes MP4 with AVFoundation in native acceptance. |

| UI-59-031 | Live editor document synchronization | partial | The Mac shell reloads a clean window within about 1-4 s after a saved-file change, honoring the MCP lock, as one Undo step that keeps surviving selection. Dirty windows keep their edits and Save still asks; sync is one way, so MCP cannot see unsaved state. docs/MCP-LIVE-CONTEXT.md. |
| UI-59-032 | Live editor selection access | partial | read_live_editor returns page, selection and viewport from an opt-in per-window sidecar beside the saved project, resolved against the saved file. Read-only, shows the latest publication, needs a saved file and an open window; source-only tool. |
| UI-59-033 | Live editor screenshot capture | partial | read_live_screenshot returns an actual WKWebView snapshot of the whole window, including panels, up to 1,600 px wide. Republished on change rather than on demand; a minimized window or background tab has none. |
| UI-59-034 | MCP raster artwork export | partial | export_live_artwork_png returns the selection or page rendered by the open editor at 1×, longest side 4,096 px. There is no headless raster export, because the server has no renderer. |
| UI-59-035 | MCP image asset import | partial | Now imports static PNG of every color type, depth and interlace, and baseline or progressive JPEG, up to 20 MB and 16 million pixels. Revision-checked with Undo. No GIF, WebP, paths or URLs; the frozen download lacks it until rebuilt. |
| UI-59-036 | MCP installed-font query | implemented | list_installed_fonts reads name tables in fixed OS font folders, returns no paths or bytes, and can compare with document fonts. macOS on-demand and .dfont fonts are not listed; runtime tested on macOS only and not cross-checked against NSFontManager's list. |
| UI-59-037 | Coordinate native editor and MCP writers | implemented | v0.2.0-dev.6: reachable workflow with appropriate core/browser/native checks and persistence/undo where applicable. See docs/VALIDATION-v0.2-grid-files-windows.md. Exact Figma reference acceptance remains separate. Native and MCP writers share the exclusive project lock; native saves compare disk SHA-256 and require an explicit choice after external changes. Live renderer synchronization and non-cooperating application locking remain outside this behavior. See docs/MAC-WINDOWS.md and mcp/README.md. |
| UI-59-038 | Durable MCP undo history | implemented | Last 20 transactions, up to 250 MB, persist across restarts in a private hidden folder beside the project, with read_undo_history and clear_undo_history. Undo is refused after any external change; no Redo; the folder keeps older content until cleared. docs/MCP-LOCAL-AUTOMATION.md. |

## Layer masks

| ID | Required behavior | Status | Verification / limits |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| UI-60-001 | Use layer as mask | implemented | v0.2.0-dev.4: reachable editing, history/persistence where applicable, core/browser checks and universal Mac build/smoke. See docs/VALIDATION-v0.2-svg-masks-selection.md; exact reference acceptance remains separate. Alpha/vector/luminance source modes mask following same-parent siblings until the next mask. Nested vector mode uses opaque geometry; bounds are conservative, and exact Figma nested-composition parity is unverified. |
